Implement the following plan:

# Ordinal Resolution LangGraph Node

## Context

Phase 3b static lookup is complete — ECOG, Karnofsky, and NYHA are recognized from the YAML config and get `unit_concept_id=8527`. But unknown ordinal scales (Child-Pugh, GCS, APACHE II, MELD, mRS, SOFA, etc.) silently pass through with `unit_concept_id=None`. Per the "Lookup → Agent → Approve → Persist" design, unknown ordinal entities should trigger an LLM agent node that proposes a mapping for human review.

## Design

Add a 7th LangGraph node `ordinal_resolve` after `structure`:

```
START → ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist → structure → ordinal_resolve → END
```

### Flow

1. **Identify candidates**: Query AtomicCriterion records from the batch where `unit_concept_id IS NULL`, `value_numeric IS NOT NULL`, and `unit_text IS NULL`. This combination (numeric value + no unit) is a strong signal for unrecognized ordinal scales. Categorical values (HIV status = "positive") are excluded because they have `value_text` not `value_numeric`.

2. **Batch LLM call**: Send all candidate entities to Gemini in a single structured-output call. Prompt asks: "For each entity, is this a clinical ordinal scoring system? If yes, propose YAML config." Uses Pydantic schema for structured output (same pattern as `detect_logic_structure()`).

3. **Immediate update**: For entities the LLM confirms as ordinal, set `unit_concept_id=8527` on the AtomicCriterion record. This is safe — `{score}` is always the correct UCUM unit for ordinal scales.

4. **Store proposals**: Write an AuditLog entry with `event_type="ordinal_scale_proposed"` containing the full proposed YAML mapping (entity aliases, LOINC codes, value ranges). This feeds the human approval workflow.

5. **Graceful degradation**: If `GOOGLE_API_KEY` is missing or the LLM fails, skip silently (same pattern as `detect_logic_structure()`). Records stay with `unit_concept_id=None` — no worse than before.

|------|--------|
| `src/protocol_processor/schemas/ordinal.py` | **NEW** — Pydantic schemas for Gemini structured output |
| `src/protocol_processor/tools/ordinal_resolver.py` | **NEW** — `resolve_ordinal_candidates()` LLM tool |
| `src/protocol_processor/nodes/ordinal_resolve.py` | **NEW** — LangGraph node function |
| `src/protocol_processor/state.py` | Add `ordinal_proposals_json: str | None` field |
| `src/protocol_processor/graph.py` | Add node + edge: `structure → ordinal_resolve → END` |
| `tests/test_ordinal_resolve.py` | **NEW** — Unit + E2E tests |
| `tests/test_graph.py` | Update node count from 6 → 7 |

class OrdinalResolutionResponse(BaseModel):

## Step 3: Node Function — `nodes/ordinal_resolve.py`

Follow the `structure_node` pattern exactly:

async def ordinal_resolve_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
```

1. Guard: return `{}` if `state.get("error")` or no `batch_id`
2. Open `Session(engine)`, query AtomicCriterion where `batch_id` matches, `unit_concept_id IS NULL`, `value_numeric IS NOT NULL`, `unit_text IS NULL`

4. Call `resolve_ordinal_candidates()` with the candidate list
5. For confirmed ordinals: `UPDATE atomic.unit_concept_id = 8527`
6. Write AuditLog with `event_type="ordinal_scale_proposed"`, `details={"proposals": [...]}`
7. Return `{"status": "completed", "errors": accumulated_errors, "ordinal_proposals_json": json.dumps(proposals)}`

## 1. `state.py` - PipelineState Definition

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/state.py`

```python
"""Pipeline state definition for the consolidated 5-node protocol processor."""

from typing import Literal
from typing_extensions import TypedDict


class PipelineState(TypedDict):
    # Input (always present)
    protocol_id: str
    file_uri: str
    title: str

    # Created by parse node
    batch_id: str | None

    # Processing artifacts (populated on-demand)
    pdf_bytes: bytes | None          # Populated by ingest, used by extract
    extraction_json: str | None      # Populated by extract, used by parse
    entities_json: str | None        # Populated by parse/ground
    grounded_entities_json: str | None  # Populated by ground, used by persist

    # Re-extraction context (optional, only present during re-extraction)
    archived_reviewed_criteria: list[dict] | None

    # Output
    status: Literal["processing", "completed", "failed"]
    error: str | None                # Fatal error — routes to END
    errors: list[str]                # Accumulated non-fatal errors
```

**Key patterns:**
- Plain `TypedDict` (no Pydantic, no SQLModel)
- Complex data uses JSON strings (`extraction_json`, `entities_json`, etc.) to minimize token usage during LangGraph state serialization
- Fatal error field (`error`, singular) vs accumulated errors (`errors`, plural list)
- All fields must be present at init; nullable fields use `| None`

## 2. `graph.py` - Node Wiring

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/graph.py`

```python
from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph
from protocol_processor.nodes.structure import structure_node
from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState


def should_continue(state: PipelineState) -> str:
    return "error" if state.get("error") else "continue"


def create_graph(checkpointer: Any = None) -> Any:
    workflow = StateGraph(PipelineState)

    # Add all 6 nodes
    workflow.add_node("ingest", ingest_node)
    workflow.add_node("extract", extract_node)
    workflow.add_node("parse", parse_node)
    workflow.add_node("ground", ground_node)
    workflow.add_node("persist", persist_node)
    workflow.add_node("structure", structure_node)

    # Entry point
    workflow.add_edge(START, "ingest")

    # Conditional error routing after ingest, extract, parse
    for source, target in [("ingest", "extract"), ("extract", "parse"), ("parse", "ground")]:
        workflow.add_conditional_edges(
            source, should_continue, {"continue": target, "error": END}
        )

    # Ground always proceeds to persist (error accumulation handles partials)
    workflow.add_edge("ground", "persist")
    # Persist proceeds to structure (expression tree building)
    workflow.add_edge("persist", "structure")
    workflow.add_edge("structure", END)

    return workflow.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)
```

**Key patterns:**
- `StateGraph(PipelineState)` — typed with the TypedDict
- `workflow.add_node("name", async_function)` — node name maps to the function
- Conditional routing only on the first 3 nodes; later nodes use plain `add_edge` + error accumulation
- Graph is compiled once, reused as a singleton via `get_graph()` (async)
- `AsyncPostgresSaver` for production checkpointing; falls back to `None` (no checkpointing) when `DATABASE_URL` is not set

## 3. `nodes/structure.py` - Node Function Pattern

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/structure.py`

```python
from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio, logging
from typing import Any
from api_service.storage import engine
from shared.models import AuditLog, Criteria
from sqlmodel import Session, select
from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
from protocol_processor.tools.structure_builder import build_expression_tree

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


async def _process_criterion(
    criterion: Criteria,
    protocol_id: str,
    session: Session,
    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
) -> str | None:
    """Returns None on success, error string on failure."""
    async with semaphore:
        try:
            # ... do work ...
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            error_msg = f"Structure build failed for criterion {criterion.id[:12]}: {e}"
            logger.error(error_msg, exc_info=True)
            return error_msg


async def structure_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span

    if state.get("error"):
        return {}   # Short-circuit if upstream fatal error

    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
    with pipeline_span("structure_node", protocol_id=protocol_id) as span:
        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": protocol_id})

        try:
            batch_id = state.get("batch_id")
            if not batch_id:
                return {"status": "completed"}

            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])

            with Session(engine) as session:
                stmt = select(Criteria).where(Criteria.batch_id == batch_id)
                all_criteria = session.exec(stmt).all()

                # ... filter, process ...

                semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
                tasks = [_process_criterion(c, protocol_id, session, semaphore) for c in qualifying]
                results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)

                for error in results:
                    if error is not None:
                        accumulated_errors.append(error)

                # Write audit log
                audit = AuditLog(
                    event_type="structure_trees_built",
                    actor_id="system:pipeline",
                    target_type="criteriabatch",
                    target_id=batch_id,
                    details={"protocol_id": protocol_id, "criteria_processed": ..., "errors": ...},
                )
                session.add(audit)
                session.commit()

            span.set_outputs({...})
            return {"status": "completed", "errors": accumulated_errors}

        except Exception as e:
            logger.exception(...)
            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
            return {
                "status": "completed",
                "errors": (state.get("errors") or []) + [f"Structure node failed: {e}"],
            }
```

**Key patterns:**
- Node signature: `async def <name>_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]`
- Return value is a **dict of state updates** (not the full state)
- Short-circuit guard: `if state.get("error"): return {}`
- Error accumulation: nodes return `{"errors": accumulated_errors}` — never `{"error": ...}` (which would be fatal)
- Private helper `_process_criterion()` returns `str | None` (None = success, string = error message) — used with `asyncio.gather()`
- `asyncio.Semaphore(4)` for parallel LLM call concurrency control
- Tracing: `with pipeline_span("node_name", protocol_id=...) as span:` wrapping the entire body
- DB: synchronous `Session(engine)` from `api_service.storage` (not async)
- AuditLog written and committed at the end of each node
- Deferred import of `pipeline_span` inside the function body

## 4. `tools/structure_builder.py` - LLM Calls with Structured Output

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/structure_builder.py`

### The `detect_logic_structure()` LLM call pattern:

```python
async def detect_logic_structure(
    criterion_text: str,
    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
) -> LogicDetectionResponse | None:
    """Returns None on any failure (triggers fallback in caller)."""
    if len(field_mappings) <= 1:
        return None   # Skip LLM for single atoms

    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    if not google_api_key:
        logger.warning("GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping ...")
        return None

    try:
        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI

        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.0-flash")
        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
            model=gemini_model_name,
            google_api_key=google_api_key,
        )
        structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(LogicDetectionResponse)

        # Build prompt from inputs
        prompt = "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. ..."

        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
        if isinstance(result, dict):
            response = LogicDetectionResponse.model_validate(result)
        else:
            response = result  # type: ignore[assignment]

        # Validate before returning
        if not _validate_logic_tree(response.root, len(field_mappings)):
            return None

        return response

    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("Logic detection failed for '...': %s", e, exc_info=True)
        return None
```

**Key patterns:**
- Late import of `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` inside `try` block
- `gemini.with_structured_output(PydanticModel)` — single call to get typed LLM output
- `os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.0-flash")` — model name from env with fallback
- Handle both `dict` and Pydantic instance return from `.with_structured_output()`
- Always return `None` on any failure (never raise) — caller provides fallback
- Validate LLM output before accepting it

### `build_expression_tree()` - Two-pass approach:

```python
async def build_expression_tree(...) -> StructuredCriterionTree:
    # Pass 1: LLM logic detection
    logic_response = await detect_logic_structure(criterion_text, field_mappings)

    if logic_response is not None:
        # Use LLM result
        return StructuredCriterionTree(root=..., structure_confidence="llm", structure_model=gemini_model_name)

    # Pass 2: Fallback — AND-of-all-atomics or single ATOMIC
    ...
    return StructuredCriterionTree(root=..., structure_confidence="fallback", structure_model=None)
```

## 5. `schemas/` - Pydantic Schema Patterns

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/schemas/structure.py`

```python
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

class LogicNode(BaseModel):
    """LLM structured output target — used directly in .with_structured_output()."""
    node_type: str = Field(description="Logic operator: 'AND', 'OR', 'NOT', or 'ATOMIC'.")
    field_mapping_index: int | None = Field(default=None, description="...")
    children: list[LogicNode] | None = Field(default=None, description="...")

class LogicDetectionResponse(BaseModel):
    """Top-level LLM output wrapper."""
    root: LogicNode = Field(description="Root node of the detected logic tree.")
    reasoning: str = Field(default="", description="Explanation of the detected logical structure.")

class ExpressionNode(BaseModel):
    """JSONB storage schema — serialized to DB via .model_dump()."""
    type: str = Field(description="Node type: 'AND', 'OR', 'NOT', or 'ATOMIC'.")
    atomic_criterion_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description="...")
    entity: str | None = Field(default=None, description="...")
    # ... all fields have defaults
    children: list[ExpressionNode] | None = Field(default=None, description="...")

class StructuredCriterionTree(BaseModel):
    """Top-level JSONB wrapper stored in Criteria.structured_criterion."""
    root: ExpressionNode = Field(description="Root node of the expression tree.")
    structure_confidence: str = Field(default="fallback", description="'llm' or 'fallback'.")
    structure_model: str | None = Field(default=None, description="...")
```

**Key patterns:**
- All `BaseModel` (not `SQLModel`) — pure Pydantic
- Every field has `Field(description=...)` — required for LLM structured output to guide the model
- Recursive models (`LogicNode.children: list[LogicNode]`) use `from __future__ import annotations`
- Two categories of schemas: **LLM output schemas** (used with `.with_structured_output()`) and **JSONB storage schemas** (serialized via `.model_dump()`)
- Stored in DB via: `criterion.structured_criterion = tree.model_dump()`
- `grounding.py` and `extraction.py` follow the same `BaseModel` + `Field(description=...)` pattern

### AtomicCriterion

```python
class AtomicCriterion(SQLModel, table=True):
    __tablename__ = "atomic_criteria"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_atomic_proto_incl", "protocol_id", "inclusion_exclusion"),
    )

    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
    criterion_id: str = Field(foreign_key="criteria.id", index=True)
    protocol_id: str = Field(foreign_key="protocol.id", index=True)
    inclusion_exclusion: str = Field()                       # "inclusion" | "exclusion"
    entity_concept_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
    entity_concept_system: str | None = Field(default=None)  # "loinc", "umls", etc.
    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
    entity_domain: str | None = Field(default=None)
    relation_operator: str | None = Field(default=None)      # ">=", "<=", "is", etc.
    value_numeric: float | None = Field(default=None, sa_column=Column(Float, nullable=True))
    value_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
    unit_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
    unit_concept_id: int | None = Field(default=None, sa_column=Column(Integer, nullable=True))
    value_concept_id: int | None = Field(default=None, sa_column=Column(Integer, nullable=True))
    negation: bool = Field(default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False))
    temporal_constraint: Dict[str, Any] | None = Field(default=None, sa_column=Column(JSON))
    original_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
    confidence_score: float | None = Field(default=None)
    human_verified: bool = Field(default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False))
    human_modified: bool = Field(default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False))
    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
```

```python
class CompositeCriterion(SQLModel, table=True):
    __tablename__ = "composite_criteria"

    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
    criterion_id: str = Field(foreign_key="criteria.id", index=True)
    protocol_id: str = Field(foreign_key="protocol.id", index=True)
    inclusion_exclusion: str = Field()
    logic_operator: str = Field()              # "AND", "OR", "NOT"
    parent_criterion_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
    original_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
    human_verified: bool = Field(...)
    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
```

### CriterionRelationship

```python
class CriterionRelationship(SQLModel, table=True):
    __tablename__ = "criterion_relationships"

    parent_criterion_id: str = Field(foreign_key="composite_criteria.id", primary_key=True)
    child_criterion_id: str = Field(primary_key=True)
    child_type: str = Field()          # "atomic" | "composite"
    child_sequence: int = Field(default=0, sa_column=Column(Integer, default=0))
```

### AuditLog

```python
class AuditLog(SQLModel, table=True):
    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
    event_type: str = Field(index=True)        # e.g. "structure_trees_built"
    actor_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)   # "system:pipeline"
    target_type: str | None = Field(default=None)             # "criteriabatch"
    target_id: str | None = Field(default=None)
    details: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, sa_column=Column(JSON))
    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
```

**Key patterns:**
- `SQLModel, table=True` for DB models (vs `BaseModel` for Pydantic-only schemas)
- UUIDs as string PKs: `default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())`
- Timestamps via `_ts_col()` / `_ts_col_update()` helpers (server-side `func.now()`)
- `sa_column=Column(JSON)` for JSONB fields
- `sa_column=Column(Float/Integer/Boolean, nullable=True)` needed when you want to override SQLModel's default nullability inference
- `__tablename__` set explicitly when table name differs from class name lowercased
- `__table_args__` for composite indexes

```python
from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState


def _make_initial_state(**overrides) -> dict:
    """Build a minimal PipelineState-compatible dict."""
    base: dict = {
        "protocol_id": "test-protocol-id",
        "file_uri": "local://test.pdf",
        "title": "Test Protocol",
        "batch_id": None,
        "pdf_bytes": None,
        "extraction_json": None,
        "entities_json": None,
        "grounded_entities_json": None,
        "status": "processing",
        "error": None,
        "errors": [],
    }
    base.update(overrides)
    return base


class TestCreateGraph:
    def test_graph_compiles(self):
        from protocol_processor.graph import create_graph
        graph = create_graph()   # No DB needed — no checkpointer
        assert graph is not None

    def test_graph_has_six_user_nodes(self):
        from protocol_processor.graph import create_graph
        graph = create_graph()
        user_nodes = {name for name in graph.nodes if not name.startswith("__")}
        assert user_nodes == {"ingest", "extract", "parse", "ground", "persist", "structure"}

    async def test_get_graph_singleton(self):
        from protocol_processor.graph import get_graph
        graph1 = await get_graph()
        graph2 = await get_graph()
        assert graph1 is graph2

class TestShouldContinue:
    def test_should_continue_no_error(self):
        from protocol_processor.graph import should_continue
        state = _make_initial_state()
        assert should_continue(state) == "continue"  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    def test_should_continue_with_error(self):
        from protocol_processor.graph import should_continue
        state = _make_initial_state(error="Something went wrong")
        assert should_continue(state) == "error"    # type: ignore[arg-type]

class TestPipelineStateShape:
    def test_state_has_required_fields(self):
        from typing import get_type_hints
        hints = get_type_hints(PipelineState)
        required = {"protocol_id", "file_uri", "title", ...}
        assert required.issubset(hints.keys())
```

**Key patterns:**
- Classes group related tests (no `unittest.TestCase` — plain classes)
- `_make_initial_state(**overrides)` helper with all required fields
- No fixtures for graph tests (no DB needed — `create_graph()` with no checkpointer)
- `# type: ignore[arg-type]` for TypedDict cast to `dict` in test calls
- Deferred imports inside test functions (`from protocol_processor.graph import ...`)
- `async def` test methods work directly (pytest-asyncio inferred)

```python
# Fixtures: in-memory SQLite
@pytest.fixture()
def engine():
    eng = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    import shared.models  # noqa: F401
    SQLModel.metadata.create_all(eng)
    try:
        yield eng
    finally:
        eng.dispose()

@pytest.fixture()
def session(engine) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    with Session(engine) as s:
        yield s

# Setup helpers
def _setup_parent(session) -> tuple[str, str]:
    protocol = Protocol(title="NCT00001234", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
    session.add(protocol); session.flush()
    batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
    session.add(batch); session.flush()
    return protocol.id, batch.id

def _make_criterion(session, batch_id, text, criteria_type="inclusion") -> Criteria:
    crit = Criteria(batch_id=batch_id, criteria_type=criteria_type, text=text)
    session.add(crit); session.flush()
    return crit

# Test: disable GOOGLE_API_KEY to skip LLM, use fallback path
class TestPhase3RealisticCriteria:
    async def test_all_criteria_processed(self, session) -> None:
        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)

        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)   # Force fallback (no LLM)

            for crit_def in CRITERIA:
                crit = _make_criterion(session, batch_id, crit_def["text"], crit_def["type"])
                await build_expression_tree(
                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
                    criterion_id=crit.id,
                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
                    inclusion_exclusion=crit_def["type"],
                    session=session,
                )
                session.flush()

                atomics = session.exec(
                    select(AtomicCriterion).where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id)
                ).all()
                all_atomics.extend(atomics)

        session.commit()

        # Verify against EXPECTED list
        for i, (atomic, exp) in enumerate(zip(all_atomics, EXPECTED)):
            assert atomic.entity_concept_id == exp["entity_concept_id"]
            assert atomic.unit_concept_id == exp["unit_concept_id"]
            assert atomic.value_concept_id == exp["value_concept_id"]
            if exp.get("value_numeric") is not None:
                assert atomic.value_numeric == pytest.approx(exp["value_numeric"])
```

**Key patterns:**
- `sqlite:///:memory:` + `StaticPool` for fully isolated in-memory DB per test session
- `import shared.models` inside the fixture to ensure all `SQLModel` subclasses are registered before `create_all()`
- `with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False): os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)` to disable LLM and force fallback path
- Test data defined as `list[dict]` constants at module level (`CRITERIA`, `EXPECTED`)
- `session.flush()` after each insert to generate IDs before use; `session.commit()` at end of test
- `pytest.approx()` for float comparisons
- `async def` test methods (no explicit `@pytest.mark.asyncio` — inferred from config)
- Direct tool function calls (`build_expression_tree`) rather than invoking the full graph — tests the tool layer in isolation

     1→"""Pipeline state definition for the consolidated 5-node protocol processor.
     2→
     3→Minimal flat state using JSON strings for complex data to minimize token usage
     4→during LangGraph state serialization between nodes.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from typing import Literal
     8→
     9→from typing_extensions import TypedDict
    10→
    11→
    12→class PipelineState(TypedDict):
    13→    """Minimal state for the protocol processing pipeline.
    14→
    15→    Fields are populated on-demand as the pipeline progresses through nodes:
    16→    ingest -> extract -> parse -> ground -> persist
    17→
    18→    Attributes:
    19→        protocol_id: UUID of the protocol being processed. Always present.
    20→        file_uri: GCS URI (gs://) or local path (local://) of the PDF.
    21→        title: Protocol title from upload metadata. Always present.
    22→        batch_id: UUID of the CriteriaBatch created by parse. None until then.
    23→        pdf_bytes: Raw PDF bytes. Populated by ingest, consumed by extract.
    24→        extraction_json: JSON string of extracted criteria from extract node.
    25→        entities_json: JSON string of entities for grounding from parse node.
    26→        grounded_entities_json: JSON string of grounded results from ground.
    27→        status: Current pipeline status. Updated by each node.
    28→        error: Fatal error message. Set on unrecoverable failure; routes to END.
    29→        errors: Accumulated non-fatal errors (e.g. individual grounding failures).
    30→            Partial failures are preserved alongside successes per user decision.
    31→    """
    32→
    33→    # Input (always present)
    34→    protocol_id: str
    35→    file_uri: str
    36→    title: str
    37→
    38→    # Created by parse node
    39→    batch_id: str | None
    40→
    41→    # Processing artifacts (populated on-demand)
    42→    pdf_bytes: bytes | None  # Populated by ingest, used by extract
    43→    extraction_json: str | None  # Populated by extract, used by parse
    44→    entities_json: str | None  # Populated by parse/ground
    45→    grounded_entities_json: str | None  # Populated by ground, used by persist
    46→
    47→    # Re-extraction context (optional, only present during re-extraction)
    48→    archived_reviewed_criteria: list[dict] | None
    49→
    50→    # Output
    51→    status: Literal["processing", "completed", "failed"]
    52→    error: str | None
    53→    errors: list[str]  # Accumulate partial failures per user decision
    54→

```
     1→"""LangGraph workflow definition for the consolidated protocol processor.
     2→
     3→Defines a 6-node StateGraph for the unified protocol processing pipeline:
     4→START -> ingest -> extract -> parse -> ground -> persist -> structure -> END
     5→
     6→Error routing:
     7→- After ingest, extract, parse: conditional edges route to END on error
     8→- After ground: always proceeds to persist (ground uses error accumulation)
     9→- Persist handles partial/total failures internally
    10→- Structure uses error accumulation (same as ground)
    11→
    12→Per user decision (v2.0 Architecture): "Flat pipeline with expression tree phase"
    13→"""
    14→
    15→import os
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.nodes.extract import extract_node
    21→from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import ground_node
    22→from protocol_processor.nodes.ingest import ingest_node
    23→from protocol_processor.nodes.parse import parse_node
    24→from protocol_processor.nodes.persist import persist_node
    25→from protocol_processor.nodes.structure import structure_node
    26→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    27→
    28→
    29→def should_continue(state: PipelineState) -> str:
    30→    """Route to error END or continue to next node.
    31→
    32→    Args:
    33→        state: Current pipeline state.
    34→
    35→    Returns:
    36→        'error' if state has a fatal error, 'continue' otherwise.
    37→    """
    38→    return "error" if state.get("error") else "continue"
    39→
    40→
    41→def create_graph(checkpointer: Any = None) -> Any:
    42→    """Create and compile the 6-node protocol processing workflow graph.
    43→
    44→    The graph follows this flow:
    45→    START -> ingest -> extract -> parse -> ground -> persist -> structure -> END
    46→
    47→    Conditional error routing after ingest, extract, and parse.
    48→    Ground always proceeds to persist (ground uses error accumulation).
    49→    Structure uses error accumulation (same pattern as ground).
    50→
    51→    Args:
    52→        checkpointer: Optional LangGraph checkpointer for fault-tolerant execution.
    53→            When provided, state is persisted after each node, enabling retry-from-
    54→            checkpoint. Use PostgresSaver for production. Defaults to None (no
    55→            checkpointing).
    56→
    57→    Returns:
    58→        Compiled StateGraph ready for async execution.
    59→    """
    60→    workflow = StateGraph(PipelineState)
    61→
    62→    # Add all 6 nodes
    63→    workflow.add_node("ingest", ingest_node)
    64→    workflow.add_node("extract", extract_node)
    65→    workflow.add_node("parse", parse_node)
    66→    workflow.add_node("ground", ground_node)
    67→    workflow.add_node("persist", persist_node)
    68→    workflow.add_node("structure", structure_node)
    69→
    70→    # Entry point
    71→    workflow.add_edge(START, "ingest")
    72→
    73→    # Conditional error routing after ingest, extract, parse
    74→    for source, target in [
    75→        ("ingest", "extract"),
    76→        ("extract", "parse"),
    77→        ("parse", "ground"),
    78→    ]:
    79→        workflow.add_conditional_edges(
    80→            source,
    81→            should_continue,
    82→            {"continue": target, "error": END},
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    # Ground always proceeds to persist (error accumulation handles partials)
    86→    workflow.add_edge("ground", "persist")
    87→    # Persist proceeds to structure (expression tree building)
    88→    workflow.add_edge("persist", "structure")
    89→    workflow.add_edge("structure", END)
    90→
    91→    return workflow.compile(checkpointer=checkpointer)
    92→
    93→
    94→# Singleton instances for reuse across outbox handler invocations.
    95→# Per Pitfall 1 from research: checkpointer is singleton, created once,
    96→# reused across all invocations. Do NOT create per-invocation.
    97→_graph = None
    98→_checkpointer = None
    99→# Context manager for PostgresSaver; kept entered for app lifetime.
   100→_checkpointer_cm: Any = None
   101→
   102→
   103→async def _get_checkpointer_async() -> Any:
   104→    """Get or create the AsyncPostgresSaver checkpointer singleton.
   105→
   106→    Creates an AsyncPostgresSaver using DATABASE_URL from the environment.
   107→    Uses the async context manager and calls asetup() to ensure checkpoint
   108→    tables exist. The context is kept open for the process lifetime.
   109→
   110→    Returns:
   111→        AsyncPostgresSaver instance configured with the application database.
   112→
   113→    Raises:
   114→        KeyError: If DATABASE_URL environment variable is not set.
   115→    """
   116→    global _checkpointer, _checkpointer_cm  # noqa: PLW0603
   117→    if _checkpointer is None:
   118→        from langgraph.checkpoint.postgres.aio import AsyncPostgresSaver
   119→
   120→        db_url = os.environ["DATABASE_URL"]
   121→        _checkpointer_cm = AsyncPostgresSaver.from_conn_string(db_url)
   122→        _checkpointer = await _checkpointer_cm.__aenter__()
   123→        await _checkpointer.asetup()  # Create checkpoint tables if they don't exist
   124→    return _checkpointer
   125→
   126→
   127→async def get_graph() -> Any:
   128→    """Get or create the compiled graph singleton.
   129→
   130→    Compiles the graph with an AsyncPostgresSaver checkpointer if DATABASE_URL
   131→    is available. Falls back to no checkpointer if DATABASE_URL is not set
   132→    (e.g. in unit tests).
   133→
   134→    Returns:
   135→        Compiled StateGraph instance.
   136→    """
   137→    global _graph  # noqa: PLW0603
   138→    if _graph is None:
   139→        try:
   140→            checkpointer = await _get_checkpointer_async()
   141→        except (KeyError, Exception):
   142→            # DATABASE_URL not set (e.g. unit tests) — compile without checkpointer
   143→            checkpointer = None
   144→        _graph = create_graph(checkpointer=checkpointer)
   145→    return _graph
   146→
```

     1→"""Structure node: build expression trees from persisted criteria.
     2→
     3→Phase 2 pipeline node that runs after persist. Reads criteria from DB,
     4→detects AND/OR/NOT logic via Gemini, creates normalized atomic_criteria/
     5→composite_criteria records, and stores the expression tree JSONB.
     6→
     7→Error handling: same accumulation pattern as ground node. Individual
     8→criterion failures don't block others. Uses asyncio.Semaphore(4) for
     9→parallel LLM calls.
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import asyncio
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from api_service.storage import engine
    19→from shared.models import AuditLog, Criteria
    20→from sqlmodel import Session, select
    21→
    22→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    23→from protocol_processor.tools.structure_builder import build_expression_tree
    24→
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→
    27→
    28→async def _process_criterion(
    29→    criterion: Criteria,
    30→    protocol_id: str,
    31→    session: Session,
    32→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
    33→) -> str | None:
    34→    """Process a single criterion: build expression tree and store it.
    35→
    36→    Args:
    37→        criterion: Criteria record with non-empty field_mappings.
    38→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for the AtomicCriterion records.
    39→        session: Active SQLModel session.
    40→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter for LLM calls.
    41→
    42→    Returns:
    43→        None on success, error message string on failure.
    44→    """
    45→    async with semaphore:
    46→        try:
    47→            conditions = criterion.conditions or {}
    48→            field_mappings = conditions.get("field_mappings", [])
    49→
    50→            if not field_mappings:
    51→                return None
    52→
    53→            inclusion_exclusion = getattr(criterion, "criteria_type", "inclusion")
    54→
    55→            tree = await build_expression_tree(
    56→                criterion_text=criterion.text,
    57→                field_mappings=field_mappings,
    58→                criterion_id=criterion.id,
    59→                protocol_id=protocol_id,
    60→                inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
    61→                session=session,
    62→            )
    63→
    64→            criterion.structured_criterion = tree.model_dump()
    65→            session.add(criterion)
    66→
    67→            logger.info(
    68→                "Built expression tree for criterion %s (confidence=%s, atoms=%d)",
    69→                criterion.id[:12],
    70→                tree.structure_confidence,
    71→                len(field_mappings),
    72→            )
    73→            return None
    74→
    75→        except Exception as e:
    76→            error_msg = f"Structure build failed for criterion {criterion.id[:12]}: {e}"
    77→            logger.error(error_msg, exc_info=True)
    78→            return error_msg
    79→
    80→
    81→async def structure_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    82→    """Build expression trees for all criteria in the batch.
    83→
    84→    Reads persisted criteria from DB (written by persist node), detects
    85→    logic structure, creates normalized table records, and stores the
    86→    expression tree JSONB on each criterion.
    87→
    88→    Args:
    89→        state: Current pipeline state with batch_id and protocol_id.
    90→
    91→    Returns:
    92→        Dict with status='completed' and accumulated errors list.
    93→    """
    94→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
    95→
    96→    if state.get("error"):
    97→        return {}
    98→
    99→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   100→    with pipeline_span("structure_node", protocol_id=protocol_id) as span:
   101→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": protocol_id})
   102→
   103→        try:
   104→            batch_id = state.get("batch_id")
   105→            if not batch_id:
   106→                logger.warning(
   107→                    "No batch_id in state for protocol %s — skipping structure",
   108→                    protocol_id,
   109→                )
   110→                span.set_outputs({"status": "completed", "note": "no batch_id"})
   111→                return {"status": "completed"}
   112→
   113→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   114→
   115→            with Session(engine) as session:
   116→                # Query criteria with non-empty field_mappings
   117→                stmt = select(Criteria).where(Criteria.batch_id == batch_id)
   118→                all_criteria = session.exec(stmt).all()
   119→
   120→                # Filter to criteria with field_mappings in conditions
   121→                qualifying = [
   122→                    c
   123→                    for c in all_criteria
   124→                    if c.conditions
   125→                    and isinstance(c.conditions, dict)
   126→                    and c.conditions.get("field_mappings")
   127→                ]
   128→
   129→                logger.info(
   130→                    "Structure node: %d/%d criteria qualify for"
   131→                    " expression tree building (protocol %s)",
   132→                    len(qualifying),
   133→                    len(all_criteria),
   134→                    protocol_id,
   135→                )
   136→
   137→                if not qualifying:
   138→                    span.set_outputs(
   139→                        {
   140→                            "status": "completed",
   141→                            "note": "no qualifying criteria",
   142→                        }
   143→                    )
   144→                    return {"status": "completed"}
   145→
   146→                # Process criteria with semaphore for LLM concurrency
   147→                semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   148→                tasks = [
   149→                    _process_criterion(c, protocol_id, session, semaphore)
   150→                    for c in qualifying
   151→                ]
   152→                results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   153→
   154→                # Collect errors
   155→                for error in results:
   156→                    if error is not None:
   157→                        accumulated_errors.append(error)
   158→
   159→                # Write audit log
   160→                audit = AuditLog(
   161→                    event_type="structure_trees_built",
   162→                    actor_id="system:pipeline",
   163→                    target_type="criteriabatch",
   164→                    target_id=batch_id,
   165→                    details={
   166→                        "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   167→                        "criteria_processed": len(qualifying),
   168→                        "errors": len([e for e in results if e is not None]),
   169→                    },
   170→                )
   171→                session.add(audit)
   172→                session.commit()
   173→
   174→            logger.info(
   175→                "Structure node complete for protocol %s:"
   176→                " %d criteria processed, %d errors",
   177→                protocol_id,
   178→                len(qualifying),
   179→                len([e for e in results if e is not None]),
   180→            )
   181→
   182→            span.set_outputs(
   183→                {
   184→                    "criteria_processed": len(qualifying),
   185→                    "errors": len([e for e in results if e is not None]),
   186→                    "status": "completed",
   187→                }
   188→            )
   189→
   190→            return {
   191→                "status": "completed",
   192→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   193→            }
   194→
   195→        except Exception as e:
   196→            logger.exception(
   197→                "Structure node failed for protocol %s: %s",
   198→                protocol_id,
   199→                e,
   200→            )
   201→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   202→            return {
   203→                "status": "completed",
   204→                "errors": (state.get("errors") or []) + [f"Structure node failed: {e}"],
   205→            }
   206→

     1→"""Structure builder tool for expression tree construction.
     2→
     3→Phase 2: Detects AND/OR/NOT logic in criterion text using Gemini,
     4→then builds normalized atomic_criteria/composite_criteria records
     5→and returns a StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage.
     6→
     7→Follows the field_mapper.py pattern: Gemini structured output with
     8→graceful fallback on failure.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, CompositeCriterion, CriterionRelationship
    18→from sqlmodel import Session
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.structure import (
    21→    ExpressionNode,
    22→    LogicDetectionResponse,
    23→    LogicNode,
    24→    StructuredCriterionTree,
    25→)
    26→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (
    27→    normalize_ordinal_value,
    28→    normalize_unit,
    29→    normalize_value,
    30→)
    31→
    32→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    33→
    34→
    35→def _parse_value(
    36→    raw_value: str,
    37→) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:
    38→    """Parse a value string into numeric or text.
    39→
    40→    Tries float conversion first. If it succeeds, returns (numeric, None).
    41→    Otherwise returns (None, text).
    42→
    43→    Args:
    44→        raw_value: Raw value string from field_mapping.
    45→
    46→    Returns:
    47→        Tuple of (value_numeric, value_text) — one is always None.
    48→    """
    49→    try:
    50→        return (float(raw_value), None)
    51→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    52→        return (None, raw_value)
    53→
    54→
    55→async def detect_logic_structure(
    56→    criterion_text: str,
    57→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
    58→) -> LogicDetectionResponse | None:
    59→    """Detect AND/OR/NOT logic structure in criterion text using Gemini.
    60→
    61→    Skips LLM call if there's only 1 field_mapping (single atom, no logic).
    62→    Returns None on any failure (triggers fallback in caller).
    63→
    64→    Args:
    65→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
    66→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
    67→
    68→    Returns:
    69→        LogicDetectionResponse with the detected tree, or None on failure.
    70→    """
    71→    if len(field_mappings) <= 1:
    72→        return None
    73→
    74→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    75→    if not google_api_key:
    76→        logger.warning(
    77→            "GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping logic detection for '%s'",
    78→            criterion_text[:50],
    79→        )
    80→        return None
    81→
    82→    try:
    83→        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    84→
    85→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.0-flash")
    86→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    87→            model=gemini_model_name,
    88→            google_api_key=google_api_key,
    89→        )
    90→        structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(LogicDetectionResponse)
    91→
    92→        # Build field mapping descriptions for context
    93→        mapping_lines = []
    94→        for i, fm in enumerate(field_mappings):
    95→            entity = fm.get("entity", "?")
    96→            relation = fm.get("relation", "?")
    97→            value = fm.get("value", "?")
    98→            unit = fm.get("unit", "")
    99→            unit_str = f" {unit}" if unit else ""
   100→            mapping_lines.append(f"  [{i}] {entity} {relation} {value}{unit_str}")
   101→        mappings_text = "\n".join(mapping_lines)
   102→
   103→        prompt = (
   104→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Analyze the logical"
   105→            " structure of this eligibility criterion.\n\n"
   106→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   107→            f"Field mappings (indexed):\n{mappings_text}\n\n"
   108→            "Instructions:\n"
   109→            "- Determine how the field mappings are logically connected\n"
   110→            "- Use AND when all conditions must be met simultaneously\n"
   111→            "- Use OR when any one condition suffices\n"
   112→            "- Use NOT to negate a condition\n"
   113→            "- Use ATOMIC for leaf nodes that reference a single"
   114→            " field_mapping by index\n"
   115→            "- Return a tree where the root is AND, OR, NOT, or ATOMIC\n"
   116→            "- Every ATOMIC node must have field_mapping_index set to a"
   117→            " valid index (0 to "
   118→            f"{len(field_mappings) - 1})\n"
   119→            "- Each field_mapping index should appear exactly once\n"
   120→        )
   121→
   122→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   123→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   124→            response = LogicDetectionResponse.model_validate(result)
   125→        else:
   126→            response = result  # type: ignore[assignment]
   127→
   128→        # Validate all field_mapping_index values are in range
   129→        if not _validate_logic_tree(response.root, len(field_mappings)):
   130→            logger.warning(
   131→                "Logic tree validation failed for '%s' — invalid indices",
   132→                criterion_text[:50],
   133→            )
   134→            return None
   135→
   136→        return response
   137→
   138→    except Exception as e:
   139→        logger.warning(
   140→            "Logic detection failed for '%s': %s",
   141→            criterion_text[:50],
   142→            e,
   143→            exc_info=True,
   144→        )
   145→        return None
   146→
   147→
   148→def _validate_logic_tree(node: LogicNode, num_mappings: int) -> bool:
   149→    """Validate that all field_mapping_index values are in range.
   150→
   151→    Args:
   152→        node: Root LogicNode to validate.
   153→        num_mappings: Number of field_mappings (valid range: 0..num_mappings-1).
   154→
   155→    Returns:
   156→        True if all indices are valid, False otherwise.
   157→    """
   158→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   159→        if node.field_mapping_index is None:
   160→            return False
   161→        return 0 <= node.field_mapping_index < num_mappings
   162→    if node.children:
   163→        return all(_validate_logic_tree(child, num_mappings) for child in node.children)
   164→    # Non-ATOMIC with no children is invalid (AND/OR/NOT need children)
   165→    return False
   166→
   167→
   168→def _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   169→    fm: dict[str, Any],
   170→    criterion_id: str,
   171→    protocol_id: str,
   172→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   173→    criterion_text: str,
   174→) -> AtomicCriterion:
   175→    """Create an AtomicCriterion record from a field_mapping dict.
   176→
   177→    Args:
   178→        fm: Field mapping dict with entity, relation, value, unit keys.
   179→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   180→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   181→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   182→        criterion_text: Original criterion text.
   183→
   184→    Returns:
   185→        AtomicCriterion instance (not yet added to session).
   186→    """
   187→    value_str = fm.get("value", "")
   188→    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)
   189→
   190→    relation = fm.get("relation", "has")
   191→    negation = relation.upper() == "NOT" if relation else False
   192→
   193→    raw_unit = fm.get("unit")
   194→    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
   195→
   196→    value_concept_id: int | None = None
   197→    entity_text = fm.get("entity")
   198→    raw_value = str(fm.get("value", ""))
   199→
   200→    # Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale
   201→    ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(raw_value, entity_text)
   202→    if ordinal_result is not None:
   203→        ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
   204→        value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
   205→        if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
   206→            unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
   207→    elif value_text and value_numeric is None:
   208→        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
   209→
   210→    return AtomicCriterion(
   211→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   212→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   213→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   214→        entity_concept_id=fm.get("entity_concept_id"),
   215→        entity_concept_system=fm.get("entity_concept_system"),
   216→        omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   217→        entity_domain=fm.get("entity_domain"),
   218→        relation_operator=relation,
   219→        value_numeric=value_numeric,
   220→        value_text=value_text,
   221→        unit_text=raw_unit,
   222→        unit_concept_id=unit_concept_id,
   223→        value_concept_id=value_concept_id,
   224→        negation=negation,
   225→        original_text=criterion_text,
   226→        confidence_score=fm.get("confidence_score"),
   227→    )
   228→
   229→
   230→def _build_tree_from_logic(
   231→    node: LogicNode,
   232→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   233→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion],
   234→    criterion_id: str,
   235→    protocol_id: str,
   236→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   237→    session: Session,
   238→) -> ExpressionNode:
   239→    """Recursively build ExpressionNode tree and DB records from LogicNode.
   240→
   241→    Creates CompositeCriterion and CriterionRelationship records for
   242→    branch nodes. Leaf nodes reference already-created AtomicCriterion
   243→    records by index.
   244→
   245→    Args:
   246→        node: Current LogicNode from LLM output.
   247→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts.
   248→        atomic_records: Pre-created AtomicCriterion records (indexed).
   249→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   250→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   251→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   252→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   253→
   254→    Returns:
   255→        ExpressionNode for JSONB storage.
   256→    """
   257→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   258→        idx = node.field_mapping_index or 0
   259→        atomic = atomic_records[idx]
   260→        fm = field_mappings[idx]
   261→        return ExpressionNode(
   262→            type="ATOMIC",
   263→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   264→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   265→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   266→            value=fm.get("value"),
   267→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   268→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   269→        )
   270→
   271→    # Branch node: create CompositeCriterion
   272→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   273→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   274→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   275→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   276→        logic_operator=node.node_type,
   277→    )
   278→    session.add(composite)
   279→    session.flush()
   280→
   281→    children_nodes: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   282→    for seq, child in enumerate(node.children or []):
   283→        child_expr = _build_tree_from_logic(
   284→            child,
   285→            field_mappings,
   286→            atomic_records,
   287→            criterion_id,
   288→            protocol_id,
   289→            inclusion_exclusion,
   290→            session,
   291→        )
   292→        children_nodes.append(child_expr)
   293→
   294→        # Create relationship edge
   295→        if child.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   296→            child_id = child_expr.atomic_criterion_id or ""
   297→            child_type = "atomic"
   298→        else:
   299→            # For composite children, the CompositeCriterion was just
   300→            # created in the recursive call — we need its ID.
   301→            # We stored it in the ExpressionNode via a convention:
   302→            # non-ATOMIC nodes don't have atomic_criterion_id set.
   303→            # Query the last composite created for this criterion.
   304→            child_id = _find_last_composite_id(session, criterion_id, child.node_type)
   305→            child_type = "composite"
   306→
   307→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   308→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   309→            child_criterion_id=child_id,
   310→            child_type=child_type,
   311→            child_sequence=seq,
   312→        )
   313→        session.add(rel)
   314→
   315→    return ExpressionNode(
   316→        type=node.node_type,
   317→        children=children_nodes,
   318→    )
   319→
   320→
   321→def _find_last_composite_id(
   322→    session: Session, criterion_id: str, logic_operator: str
   323→) -> str:
   324→    """Find the most recently added composite criterion ID.
   325→
   326→    This is used during recursive tree building to link parent→child
   327→    composite relationships.
   328→
   329→    Args:
   330→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   331→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   332→        logic_operator: Logic operator of the child composite.
   333→
   334→    Returns:
   335→        ID of the most recent CompositeCriterion matching the criteria.
   336→    """
   337→    from sqlmodel import select
   338→
   339→    stmt = (
   340→        select(CompositeCriterion)
   341→        .where(CompositeCriterion.criterion_id == criterion_id)
   342→        .where(CompositeCriterion.logic_operator == logic_operator)
   343→        .order_by(CompositeCriterion.created_at.desc())  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   344→    )
   345→    result = session.exec(stmt).first()
   346→    return result.id if result else ""
   347→
   348→
   349→async def build_expression_tree(
   350→    criterion_text: str,
   351→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   352→    criterion_id: str,
   353→    protocol_id: str,
   354→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   355→    session: Session,
   356→) -> StructuredCriterionTree:
   357→    """Build an expression tree from criterion field_mappings.
   358→
   359→    Two-pass approach:
   360→    1. LLM pass: call detect_logic_structure() for AND/OR/NOT detection
   361→    2. Fallback: if LLM fails or single mapping, AND-of-all-atomics
   362→
   363→    Creates AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping, plus
   364→    CompositeCriterion + CriterionRelationship for tree structure.
   365→
   366→    Args:
   367→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   368→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   369→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   370→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   371→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   372→        session: Active SQLModel session (records are added but not committed).
   373→
   374→    Returns:
   375→        StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage in criteria.structured_criterion.
   376→    """
   377→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.0-flash")
   378→
   379→    # Create AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping
   380→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   381→    for fm in field_mappings:
   382→        atomic = _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   383→            fm, criterion_id, protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion, criterion_text
   384→        )
   385→        session.add(atomic)
   386→        session.flush()
   387→        atomic_records.append(atomic)
   388→
   389→    # Pass 1: LLM logic detection
   390→    logic_response = await detect_logic_structure(criterion_text, field_mappings)
   391→
   392→    if logic_response is not None:
   393→        # Build tree from LLM-detected structure
   394→        root_expr = _build_tree_from_logic(
   395→            logic_response.root,
   396→            field_mappings,
   397→            atomic_records,
   398→            criterion_id,
   399→            protocol_id,
   400→            inclusion_exclusion,
   401→            session,
   402→        )
   403→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   404→            root=root_expr,
   405→            structure_confidence="llm",
   406→            structure_model=gemini_model_name,
   407→        )
   408→
   409→    # Pass 2: Fallback — AND-of-all-atomics (or single ATOMIC)
   410→    if len(atomic_records) == 1:
   411→        fm = field_mappings[0]
   412→        root_expr = ExpressionNode(
   413→            type="ATOMIC",
   414→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic_records[0].id,
   415→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   416→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   417→            value=fm.get("value"),
   418→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   419→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   420→        )
   421→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   422→            root=root_expr,
   423→            structure_confidence="fallback",
   424→            structure_model=None,
   425→        )
   426→
   427→    # Multiple mappings, LLM failed → wrap in AND
   428→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   429→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   430→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   431→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   432→        logic_operator="AND",
   433→        original_text=criterion_text,
   434→    )
   435→    session.add(composite)
   436→    session.flush()
   437→
   438→    children: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   439→    for seq, (atomic, fm) in enumerate(zip(atomic_records, field_mappings)):
   440→        children.append(
   441→            ExpressionNode(
   442→                type="ATOMIC",
   443→                atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   444→                entity=fm.get("entity"),
   445→                relation=fm.get("relation"),
   446→                value=fm.get("value"),
   447→                unit=fm.get("unit"),
   448→                omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   449→            )
   450→        )
   451→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   452→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   453→            child_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   454→            child_type="atomic",
   455→            child_sequence=seq,
   456→        )
   457→        session.add(rel)
   458→
   459→    root_expr = ExpressionNode(type="AND", children=children)
   460→    return StructuredCriterionTree(
   461→        root=root_expr,
   462→        structure_confidence="fallback",
   463→        structure_model=None,
   464→    )
   465→
```

     1→"""Pydantic schemas for expression tree logic detection and storage.
     2→
     3→Phase 2: Structured expression trees that capture AND/OR/NOT logic from
     4→criteria text. Two schema categories:
     5→
     6→1. LLM output schemas (LogicNode, LogicDetectionResponse) — Gemini structured
     7→   output for detecting logical structure in criterion text.
     8→2. JSONB storage schemas (ExpressionNode, StructuredCriterionTree) — stored
     9→   in Criteria.structured_criterion for UI rendering and CIRCE export.
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    15→
    16→
    17→class LogicNode(BaseModel):
    18→    """Tree node representing logical structure detected by Gemini.
    19→
    20→    ATOMIC nodes reference a field_mapping by index. AND/OR/NOT nodes
    21→    contain children. This schema is the Gemini structured output target.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    node_type: str = Field(
    25→        description=(
    26→            "Logic operator: 'AND', 'OR', 'NOT', or 'ATOMIC'. "
    27→            "ATOMIC nodes are leaves referencing a field_mapping."
    28→        )
    29→    )
    30→    field_mapping_index: int | None = Field(
    31→        default=None,
    32→        description=(
    33→            "Index into the field_mappings list (0-based). Only set for ATOMIC nodes."
    34→        ),
    35→    )
    36→    children: list[LogicNode] | None = Field(
    37→        default=None,
    38→        description="Child nodes for AND/OR/NOT operators. None for ATOMIC.",
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→class LogicDetectionResponse(BaseModel):
    43→    """Gemini structured output for logic detection.
    44→
    45→    Contains the root of the logic tree and the model's reasoning
    46→    about why it chose this particular logical structure.
    47→    """
    48→
    49→    root: LogicNode = Field(description="Root node of the detected logic tree.")
    50→    reasoning: str = Field(
    51→        default="",
    52→        description="Explanation of the detected logical structure.",
    53→    )
    54→
    55→
    56→class ExpressionNode(BaseModel):
    57→    """Node in the stored expression tree (JSONB in criteria.structured_criterion).
    58→
    59→    Leaf nodes carry atomic criterion details. Branch nodes carry the logic
    60→    operator and children. This is the serialization format for DB storage
    61→    and UI rendering.
    62→    """
    63→
    64→    type: str = Field(description="Node type: 'AND', 'OR', 'NOT', or 'ATOMIC'.")
    65→    atomic_criterion_id: str | None = Field(
    66→        default=None,
    67→        description="FK to atomic_criteria.id. Set for ATOMIC nodes only.",
    68→    )
    69→    entity: str | None = Field(
    70→        default=None,
    71→        description="Entity name from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    72→    )
    73→    relation: str | None = Field(
    74→        default=None,
    75→        description="Relation operator from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    76→    )
    77→    value: str | None = Field(
    78→        default=None,
    79→        description="Value from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    80→    )
    81→    unit: str | None = Field(
    82→        default=None,
    83→        description="Unit from field_mapping. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    84→    )
    85→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
    86→        default=None,
    87→        description="OMOP concept ID for this atom. Set for ATOMIC nodes.",
    88→    )
    89→    children: list[ExpressionNode] | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description="Child expression nodes. Set for AND/OR/NOT nodes.",
    92→    )
    93→
    94→
    95→class StructuredCriterionTree(BaseModel):
    96→    """Top-level wrapper stored in Criteria.structured_criterion JSONB.
    97→
    98→    Contains the expression tree root, confidence source (llm vs fallback),
    99→    and the model name used for structure detection.
   100→    """
   101→
   102→    root: ExpressionNode = Field(description="Root node of the expression tree.")
   103→    structure_confidence: str = Field(
   104→        default="fallback",
   105→        description="Source of structure: 'llm' or 'fallback'.",
   106→    )
   107→    structure_model: str | None = Field(
   108→        default=None,
   109→        description="Model name used for logic detection (e.g. 'gemini-2.0-flash').",
   110→    )
   111→

     2→

     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    12→
    13→
    14→def _make_initial_state(**overrides) -> dict:
    15→    """Build a minimal PipelineState-compatible dict."""

    17→        "protocol_id": "test-protocol-id",
    18→        "file_uri": "local://test.pdf",

    33→class TestCreateGraph:
    34→    """Tests for create_graph() compilation and structure."""

    36→    def test_graph_compiles(self):
    37→        """create_graph() should compile without raising an exception."""

    39→
    40→        graph = create_graph()

    43→    def test_graph_has_six_user_nodes(self):

    46→
    47→        graph = create_graph()
    48→        # LangGraph adds __start__ and __end__ internal nodes
    49→        # User nodes are those without leading underscores
    50→        all_nodes = graph.nodes
    51→        user_nodes = {name for name in all_nodes if not name.startswith("__")}
    52→        assert user_nodes == {
    53→            "ingest",
    54→            "extract",
    55→            "parse",
    56→            "ground",
    57→            "persist",
    58→            "structure",
    59→        }, f"Expected 6 user nodes, got: {user_nodes}"
    60→
    61→    def test_graph_node_names(self):
    62→        """Graph should contain all 6 expected node names."""
    63→        from protocol_processor.graph import create_graph
    64→
    65→        graph = create_graph()
    66→        all_nodes = graph.nodes
    67→        for expected in (
    68→            "ingest",
    69→            "extract",
    70→            "parse",
    71→            "ground",
    72→            "persist",
    73→            "structure",
    74→        ):
    75→            assert expected in all_nodes, (
    76→                f"Expected node '{expected}' not found in graph nodes: {all_nodes}"
    77→            )

    79→    async def test_get_graph_singleton(self):
    80→        """get_graph() should return the same compiled instance each call."""
    81→        from protocol_processor.graph import get_graph
    82→
    83→        graph1 = await get_graph()
    84→        graph2 = await get_graph()
    85→        assert graph1 is graph2, "get_graph() should return a singleton"

    87→    def test_graph_accepts_pipeline_state_shape(self):

    90→
    91→        graph = create_graph()

    96→        assert "structure" in graph.nodes

    99→class TestShouldContinue:
   100→    """Tests for the should_continue routing function."""
   101→
   102→    def test_should_continue_no_error(self):
   103→        """Returns 'continue' when state has no error."""
   104→        from protocol_processor.graph import should_continue
   105→
   106→        state = _make_initial_state()
   107→        assert should_continue(state) == "continue"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   108→
   109→    def test_should_continue_with_error(self):
   110→        """Returns 'error' when state has a fatal error."""
   111→        from protocol_processor.graph import should_continue
   112→
   113→        state = _make_initial_state(error="Something went wrong")
   114→        assert should_continue(state) == "error"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   115→
   116→    def test_should_continue_with_none_error(self):
   117→        """Returns 'continue' when error field is None."""
   118→        from protocol_processor.graph import should_continue
   119→
   120→        state = _make_initial_state(error=None)
   121→        assert should_continue(state) == "continue"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   122→
   123→
   124→class TestGraphErrorRouting:
   125→    """Tests for error accumulation vs fatal error routing semantics."""
   126→
   127→    def test_errors_list_not_fatal(self):
   128→        """Non-empty errors list should not trigger fatal routing."""
   129→        from protocol_processor.graph import should_continue
   130→
   131→        # errors (plural) are accumulated — not fatal
   132→        state = _make_initial_state(
   133→            errors=["entity grounding failed for 'X'"],
   134→            error=None,  # no fatal error
   135→        )
   136→        assert should_continue(state) == "continue"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   137→
   138→    def test_error_field_is_fatal(self):
   139→        """Non-empty error field (singular) triggers fatal routing."""
   140→        from protocol_processor.graph import should_continue
   141→
   142→        state = _make_initial_state(error="Parse failed: JSON decode error")
   143→        assert should_continue(state) == "error"  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   144→
   145→
   146→class TestPipelineStateShape:
   147→    """Tests for PipelineState TypedDict shape."""
   148→
   149→    def test_state_has_required_fields(self):
   150→        """PipelineState TypedDict should have all required fields."""
   151→        from typing import get_type_hints
   152→
   153→        hints = get_type_hints(PipelineState)
   154→        required = {
   155→            "protocol_id",
   156→            "file_uri",
   157→            "title",
   158→            "batch_id",
   159→            "pdf_bytes",
   160→            "extraction_json",
   161→            "entities_json",
   162→            "grounded_entities_json",
   163→            "status",
   164→            "error",
   165→            "errors",
   166→        }
   167→        assert required.issubset(hints.keys()), (
   168→            f"Missing fields in PipelineState: {required - hints.keys()}"
   169→        )
   170→
   171→    def test_errors_field_is_list(self):
   172→        """PipelineState.errors should be typed as list[str]."""
   173→        from typing import get_origin, get_type_hints
   174→
   175→        hints = get_type_hints(PipelineState)
   176→        errors_type = hints["errors"]
   177→        assert get_origin(errors_type) is list, (
   178→            f"Expected errors to be list, got: {errors_type}"
   179→        )
   180→

   139→class TestUnknownOrdinalScalesDirect:
   140→    """normalize_ordinal_value() returns None for unknown scales."""
   141→
   142→    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
   143→        "entity",
   144→        [
   145→            "Child-Pugh score",
   146→            "Glasgow Coma Scale",
   147→            "APACHE II score",
   148→            "MELD score",
   149→            "modified Rankin Scale",
   150→            "SOFA score",
   151→        ],
   152→    )
   153→    def test_unknown_scale_returns_none(self, entity: str) -> None:
   154→        result = normalize_ordinal_value("5", entity)
   155→        assert result is None, (
   156→            f"'{entity}' should NOT match any ordinal scale, "
   157→            f"but got {result}"
   158→        )
   159→
   160→
   161→class TestUnknownOrdinalScalesE2E:
   162→    """Unknown ordinal scales through build_expression_tree → DB."""
   163→
   164→    async def test_unknown_scales_get_no_unit_concept(self, session) -> None:
   165→        """Scales not in YAML get unit_concept_id=None and value_concept_id=None."""
   166→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   167→
   168→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   169→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   170→
   171→            for crit_def in UNKNOWN_ORDINAL_CRITERIA:
   172→                crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, crit_def["text"])
   173→                await build_expression_tree(
   174→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   175→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   176→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   177→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   178→                    inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   179→                    session=session,
   180→                )
   181→                session.flush()
   182→
   183→                atomics = session.exec(
   184→                    select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   185→                        AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id
   186→                    )
   187→                ).all()
   188→                assert len(atomics) == 1, f"{crit_def['label']}: expected 1 atomic"
   189→
   190→                a = atomics[0]
   191→                label = crit_def["label"]
   192→
   193→                # Key assertions: unknown ordinal scales get NO special treatment
   194→                assert a.unit_concept_id is None, (
   195→                    f"{label}: unit_concept_id should be None, got {a.unit_concept_id}"
   196→                )
   197→                assert a.value_concept_id is None, (
   198→                    f"{label}: value_concept_id should be None, got {a.value_concept_id}"
   199→                )
   200→                # But value_numeric should still parse correctly
   201→                assert a.value_numeric is not None, (
   202→                    f"{label}: value_numeric should be set"
   203→                )
   204→
   205→        session.commit()
   206→
   207→    async def test_known_vs_unknown_in_same_batch(self, session) -> None:
   208→        """ECOG (known) and Child-Pugh (unknown) in same batch behave differently."""
   209→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup(session)
   210→
   211→        criteria = [
   212→            {
   213→                "text": "ECOG <= 2",
   214→                "mappings": [
   215→                    {"entity": "ECOG", "relation": "<=", "value": "2", "unit": None},
   216→                ],
   217→            },
   218→            {
   219→                "text": "Child-Pugh <= 6",
   220→                "mappings": [
   221→                    {
   222→                        "entity": "Child-Pugh score",
   223→                        "relation": "<=",
   224→                        "value": "6",
   225→                        "unit": None,
   226→                    },
   227→                ],
   228→            },
   229→        ]
   230→
   231→        crit_ids = []
   232→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   233→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   234→
   235→            for crit_def in criteria:
   236→                crit = _make_crit(session, batch_id, crit_def["text"])
   237→                crit_ids.append(crit.id)
   238→                await build_expression_tree(
   239→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   240→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   241→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   242→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   243→                    inclusion_exclusion="inclusion",
   244→                    session=session,
   245→                )
   246→                session.flush()
   247→
   248→        session.commit()
   249→
   250→        ecog = session.exec(
   251→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   252→                AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[0]
   253→            )
   254→        ).first()
   255→        child_pugh = session.exec(
   256→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   257→                AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit_ids[1]
   258→            )
   259→        ).first()
   260→
   261→        assert ecog is not None
   262→        assert child_pugh is not None
   263→
   264→        # ECOG: recognized → gets {score} unit
   265→        assert ecog.unit_concept_id == 8527
   266→        assert ecog.value_numeric == pytest.approx(2.0)
   267→
   268→        # Child-Pugh: NOT recognized → no unit concept
   269→        assert child_pugh.unit_concept_id is None
   270→        assert child_pugh.value_numeric == pytest.approx(6.0)
