{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/schemas/ordinal.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Pydantic schemas for ordinal scale resolution via Gemini structured output.\n\nUsed by the ordinal_resolve node to identify unrecognized clinical ordinal\nscoring systems (Child-Pugh, GCS, APACHE II, etc.) and propose YAML config\nentries for human review.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n\n\nclass OrdinalValueProposal(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Proposed value mapping for a single grade within an ordinal scale.\"\"\"\n\n    grade: str = Field(\n        description=\"The grade or score value, e.g. '5', 'A', 'B'.\",\n    )\n    snomed_code: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"SNOMED CT code for this grade, if known.\",\n    )\n    loinc_answer: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"LOINC answer code for this grade, if known.\",\n    )\n    description: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Human-readable description of what this grade means.\",\n    )\n\n\nclass OrdinalScaleProposal(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"LLM proposal for a single entity that may be an ordinal scale.\"\"\"\n\n    entity_text: str = Field(\n        description=\"Original entity text from AtomicCriterion.\",\n    )\n    is_ordinal_scale: bool = Field(\n        description=\"Whether this entity is a clinical ordinal scoring system.\",\n    )\n    confidence: float = Field(\n        description=\"Confidence score from 0.0 to 1.0.\",\n    )\n    scale_name: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Snake_case key for YAML config, e.g. 'child_pugh'.\",\n    )\n    entity_aliases: list[str] = Field(\n        default_factory=list,\n        description=\"Alternative names for this scale.\",\n    )\n    loinc_code: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"LOINC code for this ordinal scale, if known.\",\n    )\n    unit_concept_id: int = Field(\n        default=8527,\n        description=\"UCUM unit concept ID. Always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.\",\n    )\n    values: list[OrdinalValueProposal] = Field(\n        default_factory=list,\n        description=\"Proposed value mappings for grades within this scale.\",\n    )\n    reasoning: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Explanation of why this entity is/isn't an ordinal scale.\",\n    )\n\n\nclass OrdinalResolutionResponse(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Top-level Gemini structured output for ordinal resolution.\"\"\"\n\n    proposals: list[OrdinalScaleProposal] = Field(\n        description=\"One proposal per candidate entity.\",\n    )\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/ordinal_resolver.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Ordinal scale resolution tool using Gemini structured output.\n\nIdentifies unrecognized clinical ordinal scoring systems (Child-Pugh, GCS,\nAPACHE II, MELD, mRS, SOFA, etc.) and proposes YAML config entries for\nhuman review.\n\nFollows the detect_logic_structure() pattern from structure_builder.py:\n- Guard on GOOGLE_API_KEY\n- Late import of ChatGoogleGenerativeAI\n- with_structured_output() for typed LLM response\n- Return None on any failure (graceful degradation)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom protocol_processor.schemas.ordinal import (\n    OrdinalResolutionResponse,\n    OrdinalScaleProposal,\n)\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nasync def resolve_ordinal_candidates(\n    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]],\n) -> OrdinalResolutionResponse | None:\n    \"\"\"Send candidate entities to Gemini for ordinal scale identification.\n\n    Args:\n        candidates: List of dicts with keys 'entity_text', 'value_numeric',\n            'relation_operator' from AtomicCriterion records.\n\n    Returns:\n        OrdinalResolutionResponse with proposals, or None on failure/skip.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None\n\n    google_api_key = os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\")\n    if not google_api_key:\n        logger.warning(\n            \"GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping ordinal resolution\"\n        )\n        return None\n\n    try:\n        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI\n\n        gemini_model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.0-flash\")\n        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(\n            model=gemini_model_name,\n            google_api_key=google_api_key,\n        )\n        structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(OrdinalResolutionResponse)\n\n        # Build indexed entity list for the prompt\n        entity_lines = []\n        for i, c in enumerate(candidates):\n            entity = c.get(\"entity_text\", \"?\")\n            value = c.get(\"value_numeric\", \"?\")\n            relation = c.get(\"relation_operator\", \"?\")\n            entity_lines.append(\n                f\"  [{i}] entity=\\\"{entity}\\\" relation=\\\"{relation}\\\" value={value}\"\n            )\n        entities_text = \"\\n\".join(entity_lines)\n\n        prompt = (\n            \"You are a clinical terminology expert specializing in medical scoring \"\n            \"systems and ordinal scales.\\n\\n\"\n            \"For each entity below, determine if it is a clinical ordinal scoring \"\n            \"system (e.g. ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, Child-Pugh, GCS, APACHE II, MELD, \"\n            \"mRS, SOFA, etc.).\\n\\n\"\n            \"An ordinal scoring system has these characteristics:\\n\"\n            \"- Uses discrete numeric grades/scores (not continuous measurements)\\n\"\n            \"- Each grade has a specific clinical meaning\\n\"\n            \"- The unit is conceptually {score}, not a physical unit\\n\"\n            \"- Common in clinical trials for performance status, organ function, \"\n            \"disease severity\\n\\n\"\n            \"NOT ordinal scales:\\n\"\n            \"- Lab values with physical units (mg/dL, g/L, IU/mL)\\n\"\n            \"- Continuous measurements (weight, height, BMI)\\n\"\n            \"- Binary yes/no conditions (HIV status, pregnancy)\\n\"\n            \"- Age, duration, or time-based criteria\\n\\n\"\n            f\"Entities to evaluate:\\n{entities_text}\\n\\n\"\n            \"For each entity that IS an ordinal scale, propose:\\n\"\n            \"- scale_name: snake_case identifier for YAML config\\n\"\n            \"- entity_aliases: alternative names clinicians might use\\n\"\n            \"- loinc_code: LOINC code if known\\n\"\n            \"- values: list of grades with descriptions\\n\"\n        )\n\n        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)\n        if isinstance(result, dict):\n            response = OrdinalResolutionResponse.model_validate(result)\n        else:\n            response = result  # type: ignore[assignment]\n\n        # Filter to confirmed ordinals with sufficient confidence\n        confirmed: list[OrdinalScaleProposal] = [\n            p for p in response.proposals\n            if p.is_ordinal_scale and p.confidence >= 0.7\n        ]\n\n        return OrdinalResolutionResponse(proposals=confirmed)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\n            \"Ordinal resolution failed: %s\",\n            e,\n            exc_info=True,\n        )\n        return None\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ordinal_resolve.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Ordinal resolve node: identify and update unrecognized ordinal scales.\n\n7th pipeline node that runs after structure. Queries AtomicCriterion records\nwhere unit_concept_id IS NULL, value_numeric IS NOT NULL, and unit_text IS NULL\n(strong signal for unrecognized ordinal scales). Sends candidates to Gemini\nfor identification, updates confirmed ordinals with unit_concept_id=8527,\nand writes AuditLog proposals for human review.\n\nError handling: same accumulation pattern as structure node. LLM failures\ndon't block the pipeline — records stay with unit_concept_id=None.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom api_service.storage import engine\nfrom shared.models import AtomicCriterion, AuditLog\nfrom sqlmodel import Session, select\n\nfrom protocol_processor.state import PipelineState\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.ordinal_resolver import resolve_ordinal_candidates\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nasync def ordinal_resolve_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Identify unrecognized ordinal scales and update their unit_concept_id.\n\n    Queries AtomicCriterion records from the batch where unit_concept_id is\n    NULL, value_numeric is set, and unit_text is NULL. Sends deduplicated\n    candidates to Gemini for ordinal scale identification. Updates confirmed\n    ordinals with unit_concept_id=8527 ({score}).\n\n    Args:\n        state: Current pipeline state with batch_id and protocol_id.\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with status='completed', accumulated errors, and ordinal_proposals_json.\n    \"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span\n\n    if state.get(\"error\"):\n        return {}\n\n    protocol_id = state.get(\"protocol_id\", \"\")\n    with pipeline_span(\"ordinal_resolve_node\", protocol_id=protocol_id) as span:\n        span.set_inputs({\"protocol_id\": protocol_id})\n\n        try:\n            batch_id = state.get(\"batch_id\")\n            if not batch_id:\n                logger.warning(\n                    \"No batch_id in state for protocol %s — skipping ordinal resolve\",\n                    protocol_id,\n                )\n                span.set_outputs({\"status\": \"completed\", \"note\": \"no batch_id\"})\n                return {\"status\": \"completed\"}\n\n            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get(\"errors\") or [])\n\n            with Session(engine) as session:\n                # Query AtomicCriterion where unit_concept_id IS NULL,\n                # value_numeric IS NOT NULL, and unit_text IS NULL\n                stmt = (\n                    select(AtomicCriterion)\n                    .where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id.in_(  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                        select(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id)\n                        .where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id.isnot(None))  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                    ))\n                    .where(\n                        AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                        AtomicCriterion.value_numeric.isnot(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                        AtomicCriterion.unit_text.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                    )\n                )\n\n                # Filter by batch: join through criterion_id to criteria with matching batch_id\n                from shared.models import Criteria\n\n                stmt = (\n                    select(AtomicCriterion)\n                    .join(Criteria, AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == Criteria.id)\n                    .where(\n                        Criteria.batch_id == batch_id,\n                        AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                        AtomicCriterion.value_numeric.isnot(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                        AtomicCriterion.unit_text.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]\n                    )\n                )\n                candidate_records = session.exec(stmt).all()\n\n                if not candidate_records:\n                    logger.info(\n                        \"No ordinal candidates found for protocol %s\",\n                        protocol_id,\n                    )\n                    span.set_outputs(\n                        {\"status\": \"completed\", \"candidates\": 0}\n                    )\n                    return {\"status\": \"completed\", \"errors\": accumulated_errors}\n\n                # Deduplicate by entity text (original_text may contain full criterion)\n                seen_entities: set[str] = set()\n                unique_candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n                for rec in candidate_records:\n                    # Use entity from the original field_mapping via concept_id lookup\n                    # The entity_concept_id field stores the entity concept, but\n                    # original_text has the full criterion text. We need to extract\n                    # the entity name. Use a heuristic: the entity is derived from\n                    # the original_text before the relation_operator.\n                    entity_text = _extract_entity_text(rec)\n                    if entity_text and entity_text not in seen_entities:\n                        seen_entities.add(entity_text)\n                        unique_candidates.append({\n                            \"entity_text\": entity_text,\n                            \"value_numeric\": rec.value_numeric,\n                            \"relation_operator\": rec.relation_operator,\n                        })\n\n                logger.info(\n                    \"Ordinal resolve: %d candidates (%d unique entities) \"\n                    \"for protocol %s\",\n                    len(candidate_records),\n                    len(unique_candidates),\n                    protocol_id,\n                )\n\n                # Call LLM for ordinal resolution\n                resolution = await resolve_ordinal_candidates(unique_candidates)\n\n                proposals_data: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n                updated_count = 0\n\n                if resolution and resolution.proposals:\n                    # Build lookup of confirmed ordinal entity texts\n                    confirmed_entities: set[str] = set()\n                    for proposal in resolution.proposals:\n                        confirmed_entities.add(proposal.entity_text)\n                        proposals_data.append(proposal.model_dump())\n\n                    # Update matching AtomicCriterion records\n                    for rec in candidate_records:\n                        entity_text = _extract_entity_text(rec)\n                        if entity_text and entity_text in confirmed_entities:\n                            rec.unit_concept_id = 8527\n                            session.add(rec)\n                            updated_count += 1\n\n                # Write audit log with proposals\n                audit = AuditLog(\n                    event_type=\"ordinal_scale_proposed\",\n                    actor_id=\"system:pipeline\",\n                    target_type=\"criteriabatch\",\n                    target_id=batch_id,\n                    details={\n                        \"protocol_id\": protocol_id,\n                        \"candidates_found\": len(candidate_records),\n                        \"unique_entities\": len(unique_candidates),\n                        \"proposals\": proposals_data,\n                        \"updated_count\": updated_count,\n                    },\n                )\n                session.add(audit)\n                session.commit()\n\n            logger.info(\n                \"Ordinal resolve complete for protocol %s: \"\n                \"%d candidates, %d updated, %d proposals\",\n                protocol_id,\n                len(candidate_records),\n                updated_count,\n                len(proposals_data),\n            )\n\n            proposals_json = json.dumps(proposals_data) if proposals_data else None\n\n            span.set_outputs({\n                \"candidates\": len(candidate_records),\n                \"updated\": updated_count,\n                \"proposals\": len(proposals_data),\n                \"status\": \"completed\",\n            })\n\n            return {\n                \"status\": \"completed\",\n                \"errors\": accumulated_errors,\n                \"ordinal_proposals_json\": proposals_json,\n            }\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(\n                \"Ordinal resolve node failed for protocol %s: %s\",\n                protocol_id,\n                e,\n            )\n            span.set_outputs({\"error\": str(e)})\n            return {\n                \"status\": \"completed\",\n                \"errors\": (state.get(\"errors\") or [])\n                + [f\"Ordinal resolve node failed: {e}\"],\n            }\n\n\ndef _extract_entity_text(rec: AtomicCriterion) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Extract a usable entity identifier from an AtomicCriterion record.\n\n    Tries entity_concept_id first (most reliable), then falls back to\n    parsing original_text before the relation_operator.\n\n    Args:\n        rec: AtomicCriterion record.\n\n    Returns:\n        Entity text string, or None if not extractable.\n    \"\"\"\n    # entity_concept_id stores the concept code, but for display/matching\n    # we need the human-readable name. The original_text contains the full\n    # criterion, and entity info is encoded in entity_concept_id/entity_domain.\n    # Best heuristic: parse original_text up to the relation operator.\n    if rec.original_text and rec.relation_operator:\n        # e.g. \"Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)\" → \"Child-Pugh score\"\n        parts = rec.original_text.split(rec.relation_operator, 1)\n        if parts:\n            return parts[0].strip()\n\n    # Fallback: use original_text as-is (may be the full criterion)\n    return rec.original_text.strip() if rec.original_text else None\n"
}

\"\"\"Unit tests for the LLM tool function.\"\"\"\n\n    async def test_no_candidates_skips_llm(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Empty candidate list returns None without calling LLM.\"\"\"\n      assert len(audit.details[\"proposals\"]) == 1\n        assert audit.details[\"proposals\"][0][\"scale_name\"] == \"gcs\"\n\n    async def test_proposals_in_state(self, engine, session) -> None:\n        \"\"\"ordinal_proposals_json populated in returned state.\"\"\"\n        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)\n        crit = _make_criterion(session, batch_id, \"APACHE II score < 25\")\n        _make_atomic(\n            session, crit.id, protocol_id,\n            original_text=\"APACHE II score < 25\",\n            relation_operator=\"<\", value_numeric=25.0,\n            unit_text=None, unit_concept_id=None,\n        )\n        session.commit()\n\n        mock_response = _build_mock_response([\n            {\n                \"entity_text\": \"APACHE II score\",\n                \"is_ordinal_scale\": True,\n                \"confidence\": 0.88,\n                \"scale_name\": \"apache_ii\",\n            },\n        ])\n\n        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node\n\n        state = {\n            \"protocol_id\": protocol_id,\n            \"batch_id\": batch_id,\n            \"error\": None,\n            \"errors\": [],\n        }\n\n        with (\n            patch(\"protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine\", engine),\n            patch(\n                \"protocol_processor.tools.ordinal_resolver.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI\"\n            ) as mock_cls,\n            patch.dict(os.environ, {\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\": \"test-key\"}),\n        ):\n            mock_model = mock_cls.return_value\n            mock_structured = mock_model.with_structured_output.return_value\n            mock_structured.invoke.return_value = mock_response\n\n            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n        assert result.get(\"ordinal_proposals_json\") is not None\n        proposals = json.loads(result[\"ordinal_proposals_json\"])\n        assert len(proposals) == 1\n        assert proposals[0][\"scale_name\"] == \"apache_ii\"\n\n    async def test_no_candidates_no_llm_call(self, engine, session) -> None:\n        \"\"\"No unresolved ordinal candidates → no LLM call, no proposals.\"\"\"\n        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)\n        # All atomics have unit_concept_id set\n        crit = _make_criterion(session, batch_id, \"ECOG <= 2\")\n        _make_atomic(\n            session, crit.id, protocol_id,\n            original_text=\"ECOG <= 2\",\n            relation_operator=\"<=\", value_numeric=2.0,\n            unit_concept_id=8527,\n        )\n        session.commit()\n\n        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node\n\n        state = {\n            \"protocol_id\": protocol_id,\n            \"batch_id\": batch_id,\n            \"error\": None,\n            \"errors\": [],\n        }\n\n        mock_resolve = AsyncMock(return_value=None)\n        with (\n            patch(\"protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine\", engine),\n            patch(\n                \"protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.resolve_ordinal_candidates\",\n                mock_resolve,\n            ),\n        ):\n            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n        assert result[\"status\"] == \"completed\"\n        mock_resolve.assert_not_called()\n        assert result.get(\"ordinal_proposals_json\") is None\n\n    async def test_error_state_short_circuits(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Node returns empty dict when state has fatal error.\"\"\"\n        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node\n\n        state = {\n            \"protocol_id\": \"test\",\n            \"batch_id\": \"test-batch\",\n            \"error\": \"upstream failure\",\n            \"errors\": [],\n        }\n        result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n        assert result == {}\n\n    async def test_no_batch_id_skips(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Node skips gracefully when batch_id is missing.\"\"\"\n        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node\n\n        state = {\n            \"protocol_id\": \"test\",\n            \"batch_id\": None,\n            \"error\": None,\n            \"errors\": [],\n        }\n        result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n        assert result[\"status\"] == \"completed\"\n"

     1→"""Ordinal resolve node: identify and update unrecognized ordinal scales.
     2→
     3→7th pipeline node that runs after structure. Queries AtomicCriterion records
     4→where unit_concept_id IS NULL, value_numeric IS NOT NULL, and unit_text IS NULL
     5→(strong signal for unrecognized ordinal scales). Sends candidates to Gemini
     6→for identification, updates confirmed ordinals with unit_concept_id=8527,
     7→and writes AuditLog proposals for human review.
     8→
     9→Error handling: same accumulation pattern as structure node. LLM failures
    10→don't block the pipeline — records stay with unit_concept_id=None.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import json
    16→import logging
    17→from typing import Any
    18→
    19→from api_service.storage import engine
    20→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, AuditLog
    21→from sqlmodel import Session, select
    22→
    23→from protocol_processor.state import PipelineState
    24→from protocol_processor.tools.ordinal_resolver import resolve_ordinal_candidates
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→
    29→async def ordinal_resolve_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    30→    """Identify unrecognized ordinal scales and update their unit_concept_id.
    31→
    32→    Queries AtomicCriterion records from the batch where unit_concept_id is
    33→    NULL, value_numeric is set, and unit_text is NULL. Sends deduplicated
    34→    candidates to Gemini for ordinal scale identification. Updates confirmed
    35→    ordinals with unit_concept_id=8527 ({score}).
    36→
    37→    Args:
    38→        state: Current pipeline state with batch_id and protocol_id.
    39→
    40→    Returns:
    41→        Dict with status='completed', accumulated errors, and ordinal_proposals_json.
    42→    """
    43→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
    44→
    45→    if state.get("error"):
    46→        return {}
    47→
    48→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
    49→    with pipeline_span("ordinal_resolve_node", protocol_id=protocol_id) as span:
    50→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": protocol_id})
    51→
    52→        try:
    53→            batch_id = state.get("batch_id")
    54→            if not batch_id:
    55→                logger.warning(
    56→                    "No batch_id in state for protocol %s — skipping ordinal resolve",
    57→                    protocol_id,
    58→                )
    59→                span.set_outputs({"status": "completed", "note": "no batch_id"})
    60→                return {"status": "completed"}
    61→
    62→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
    63→
    64→            with Session(engine) as session:
    65→                # Query AtomicCriterion where unit_concept_id IS NULL,
    66→                # value_numeric IS NOT NULL, and unit_text IS NULL
    67→                stmt = (
    68→                    select(AtomicCriterion)
    69→                    .where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id.in_(  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    70→                        select(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id)
    71→                        .where(AtomicCriterion.criterion_id.isnot(None))  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    72→                    ))
    73→                    .where(
    74→                        AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    75→                        AtomicCriterion.value_numeric.isnot(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    76→                        AtomicCriterion.unit_text.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    77→                    )
    78→                )
    79→
    80→                # Filter by batch: join through criterion_id to criteria with matching batch_id
    81→                from shared.models import Criteria
    82→
    83→                stmt = (
    84→                    select(AtomicCriterion)
    85→                    .join(Criteria, AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == Criteria.id)
    86→                    .where(
    87→                        Criteria.batch_id == batch_id,
    88→                        AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    89→                        AtomicCriterion.value_numeric.isnot(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    90→                        AtomicCriterion.unit_text.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    91→                    )
    92→                )
    93→                candidate_records = session.exec(stmt).all()
    94→
    95→                if not candidate_records:
    96→                    logger.info(
    97→                        "No ordinal candidates found for protocol %s",
    98→                        protocol_id,
    99→                    )
   100→                    span.set_outputs(
   101→                        {"status": "completed", "candidates": 0}
   102→                    )
   103→                    return {"status": "completed", "errors": accumulated_errors}
   104→
   105→                # Deduplicate by entity text (original_text may contain full criterion)
   106→                seen_entities: set[str] = set()
   107→                unique_candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   108→                for rec in candidate_records:
   109→                    # Use entity from the original field_mapping via concept_id lookup
   110→                    # The entity_concept_id field stores the entity concept, but
   111→                    # original_text has the full criterion text. We need to extract
   112→                    # the entity name. Use a heuristic: the entity is derived from
   113→                    # the original_text before the relation_operator.
   114→                    entity_text = _extract_entity_text(rec)
   115→                    if entity_text and entity_text not in seen_entities:
   116→                        seen_entities.add(entity_text)
   117→                        unique_candidates.append({
   118→                            "entity_text": entity_text,
   119→                            "value_numeric": rec.value_numeric,
   120→                            "relation_operator": rec.relation_operator,
   121→                        })
   122→
   123→                logger.info(
   124→                    "Ordinal resolve: %d candidates (%d unique entities) "
   125→                    "for protocol %s",
   126→                    len(candidate_records),
   127→                    len(unique_candidates),
   128→                    protocol_id,
   129→                )
   130→
   131→                # Call LLM for ordinal resolution
   132→                resolution = await resolve_ordinal_candidates(unique_candidates)
   133→
   134→                proposals_data: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   135→                updated_count = 0
   136→
   137→                if resolution and resolution.proposals:
   138→                    # Build lookup of confirmed ordinal entity texts
   139→                    confirmed_entities: set[str] = set()
   140→                    for proposal in resolution.proposals:
   141→                        confirmed_entities.add(proposal.entity_text)
   142→                        proposals_data.append(proposal.model_dump())
   143→
   144→                    # Update matching AtomicCriterion records
   145→                    for rec in candidate_records:
   146→                        entity_text = _extract_entity_text(rec)
   147→                        if entity_text and entity_text in confirmed_entities:
   148→                            rec.unit_concept_id = 8527
   149→                            session.add(rec)
   150→                            updated_count += 1
   151→
   152→                # Write audit log with proposals
   153→                audit = AuditLog(
   154→                    event_type="ordinal_scale_proposed",
   155→                    actor_id="system:pipeline",
   156→                    target_type="criteriabatch",
   157→                    target_id=batch_id,
   158→                    details={
   159→                        "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   160→                        "candidates_found": len(candidate_records),
   161→                        "unique_entities": len(unique_candidates),
   162→                        "proposals": proposals_data,
   163→                        "updated_count": updated_count,
   164→                    },
   165→                )
   166→                session.add(audit)
   167→                session.commit()
   168→
   169→            logger.info(
   170→                "Ordinal resolve complete for protocol %s: "
   171→                "%d candidates, %d updated, %d proposals",
   172→                protocol_id,
   173→                len(candidate_records),
   174→                updated_count,
   175→                len(proposals_data),
   176→            )
   177→
   178→            proposals_json = json.dumps(proposals_data) if proposals_data else None
   179→
   180→            span.set_outputs({
   181→                "candidates": len(candidate_records),
   182→                "updated": updated_count,
   183→                "proposals": len(proposals_data),
   184→                "status": "completed",
   185→            })
   186→
   187→            return {
   188→                "status": "completed",
   189→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   190→                "ordinal_proposals_json": proposals_json,
   191→            }
   192→
   193→        except Exception as e:
   194→            logger.exception(
   195→                "Ordinal resolve node failed for protocol %s: %s",
   196→                protocol_id,
   197→                e,
   198→            )
   199→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   200→            return {
   201→                "status": "completed",
   202→                "errors": (state.get("errors") or [])
   203→                + [f"Ordinal resolve node failed: {e}"],
   204→            }
   205→
   206→
   207→def _extract_entity_text(rec: AtomicCriterion) -> str | None:
   208→    """Extract a usable entity identifier from an AtomicCriterion record.
   209→
   210→    Tries entity_concept_id first (most reliable), then falls back to
   211→    parsing original_text before the relation_operator.
   212→
   213→    Args:
   214→        rec: AtomicCriterion record.
   215→
   216→    Returns:
   217→        Entity text string, or None if not extractable.
   218→    """
   219→    # entity_concept_id stores the concept code, but for display/matching
   220→    # we need the human-readable name. The original_text contains the full
   221→    # criterion, and entity info is encoded in entity_concept_id/entity_domain.
   222→    # Best heuristic: parse original_text up to the relation operator.
   223→    if rec.original_text and rec.relation_operator:
   224→        # e.g. "Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)" → "Child-Pugh score"
   225→        parts = rec.original_text.split(rec.relation_operator, 1)
   226→        if parts:
   227→            return parts[0].strip()
   228→
   229→    # Fallback: use original_text as-is (may be the full criterion)
   230→    return rec.original_text.strip() if rec.original_text else None
   231→

    59→def _setup_parent(session: Session) -> tuple[str, str]:
    60→    """Create Protocol + CriteriaBatch, return (protocol_id, batch_id)."""
    61→    protocol = Protocol(title="NCT-ORDINAL-TEST", file_uri="local://test.pdf")

    66→    session.flush()
    67→    return protocol.id, batch.id

    72→) -> Criteria:
    73→    crit = Criteria(batch_id=batch_id, criteria_type=criteria_type, text=text)
    74→    session.add(crit)
    75→    session.flush()
    76→    return crit

   268→class TestOrdinalResolveNodeE2E:

   271→    async def test_child_pugh_gets_resolved(self, engine, session) -> None:
   272→        """Child-Pugh criterion with mocked LLM → unit_concept_id updated to 8527."""
   273→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   274→        crit = _make_criterion(session, batch_id, "Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)")
   275→        atomic = _make_atomic(
   276→            session,
   277→            crit.id,
   278→            protocol_id,
   279→            original_text="Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)",
   280→            relation_operator="<=",
   281→            value_numeric=6.0,
   282→            unit_text=None,
   283→            unit_concept_id=None,
   284→        )
   285→        session.commit()
   286→
   287→        mock_response = _build_mock_response([
   288→            {
   289→                "entity_text": "Child-Pugh score",
   290→                "is_ordinal_scale": True,
   291→                "confidence": 0.95,
   292→                "scale_name": "child_pugh",
   293→                "entity_aliases": ["Child-Pugh"],
   294→                "loinc_code": "75622-1",
   295→            },
   296→        ])
   297→
   298→        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node
   299→
   300→        state = {
   301→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   302→            "batch_id": batch_id,
   303→            "error": None,
   304→            "errors": [],
   305→        }
   306→
   307→        with (
   308→            patch("protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine", engine),
   309→            patch(
   310→                "protocol_processor.tools.ordinal_resolver.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI"
   311→            ) as mock_cls,
   312→            patch.dict(os.environ, {"GOOGLE_API_KEY": "test-key"}),
   313→        ):
   314→            mock_model = mock_cls.return_value
   315→            mock_structured = mock_model.with_structured_output.return_value
   316→            mock_structured.invoke.return_value = mock_response
   317→
   318→            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   319→
   320→        assert result["status"] == "completed"
   321→
   322→        # Verify the AtomicCriterion was updated
   323→        session.expire_all()
   324→        updated = session.get(AtomicCriterion, atomic.id)
   325→        assert updated is not None
   326→        assert updated.unit_concept_id == 8527

   367→    async def test_mixed_batch(self, engine, session) -> None:
   368→        """Batch with ECOG + Child-Pugh + HbA1c → only Child-Pugh sent to LLM."""
   369→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)

   388→
   389→        # Child-Pugh: unresolved ordinal candidate
   390→        crit3 = _make_criterion(session, batch_id, "Child-Pugh score <= 6")
   391→        child_pugh_atomic = _make_atomic(
   392→            session, crit3.id, protocol_id,
   393→            original_text="Child-Pugh score <= 6",
   394→            relation_operator="<=", value_numeric=6.0,
   395→            unit_text=None, unit_concept_id=None,
   396→        )
   397→        session.commit()
   398→
   399→        mock_response = _build_mock_response([
   400→            {
   401→                "entity_text": "Child-Pugh score",
   402→                "is_ordinal_scale": True,
   403→                "confidence": 0.95,
   404→                "scale_name": "child_pugh",
   405→            },
   406→        ])
   407→
   408→        from protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import ordinal_resolve_node
   409→
   410→        state = {
   411→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   412→            "batch_id": batch_id,
   413→            "error": None,
   414→            "errors": [],
   415→        }
   416→
   417→        with (
   418→            patch("protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine", engine),
   419→            patch(
   420→                "protocol_processor.tools.ordinal_resolver.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI"
   421→            ) as mock_cls,
   422→            patch.dict(os.environ, {"GOOGLE_API_KEY": "test-key"}),
   423→        ):
   424→            mock_model = mock_cls.return_value
   425→            mock_structured = mock_model.with_structured_output.return_value
   426→            mock_structured.invoke.return_value = mock_response
   427→
   428→            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   429→
   430→        assert result["status"] == "completed"
   431→
   432→        # Only Child-Pugh should have been updated
   433→        session.expire_all()
   434→        updated = session.get(AtomicCriterion, child_pugh_atomic.id)
   435→        assert updated is not None
   436→        assert updated.unit_concept_id == 8527
   437→
   438→        # Verify the LLM was called with only the Child-Pugh entity
   439→        call_args = mock_structured.invoke.call_args
   440→        prompt_text = call_args[0][0]
   441→        assert "Child-Pugh score" in prompt_text
   442→        # ECOG and HbA1c should NOT appear in the LLM prompt
   443→        assert "ECOG" not in prompt_text
   444→        assert "HbA1c" not in prompt_text

     1→"""Unit and value normalizer: UCUM lookup with OMOP concept ID resolution.
     2→
     3→Phase 3 (Gap 7): Deterministic YAML-based lookup mapping common clinical
     4→trial units to UCUM codes and OMOP unit_concept_id values, plus optional
     5→categorical value normalization (e.g. "positive" -> SNOMED qualifier concept).
     6→
     7→Phase 3b: Ordinal scale normalization (ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, etc.) with
     8→agent-assisted concept resolution.  Recognized ordinal entities get
     9→unit_concept_id=8527 ({score}); value_concept_id comes from YAML config
    10→(populated incrementally via agent/approval workflow).
    11→
    12→No LLM calls — pure static lookup with alias expansion and case-insensitive
    13→matching.  Returns (None, None) for unrecognized inputs; never raises.
    14→"""
    15→
    16→from __future__ import annotations
    17→
    18→import logging
    19→import re
    20→from functools import lru_cache
    21→from pathlib import Path
    22→from typing import Any
    23→
    24→import yaml
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config" / "ucum_mappings.yaml"
    29→
    30→_ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(
    31→    r"^(?:grade|stage|class|score|level)\s*",
    32→    re.IGNORECASE,
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    37→def _load_ucum_mappings() -> tuple[dict[str, tuple[str, int]], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Load and index the UCUM mappings YAML (cached after first call).
    39→
    40→    Returns:
    41→        Tuple of (unit_lookup, value_lookup) where:
    42→        - unit_lookup: lowercased alias/canonical -> (canonical, omop_id)
    43→        - value_lookup: lowercased value text -> omop_value_concept_id
    44→    """
    45→    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
    46→        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)
    47→
    48→    unit_lookup: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}
    49→    for entry in data.get("units", []):
    50→        canonical: str = entry["canonical"]
    51→        omop_id: int = entry["omop_unit_concept_id"]
    52→        # Index canonical form
    53→        unit_lookup[canonical.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    54→        # Index all aliases
    55→        for alias in entry.get("aliases", []):
    56→            unit_lookup[alias.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    57→
    58→    value_lookup: dict[str, int] = {}
    59→    for text, concept_id in data.get("value_mappings", {}).items():
    60→        value_lookup[str(text).strip().lower()] = int(concept_id)
    61→
    62→    return unit_lookup, value_lookup
    63→
    64→
    65→def normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    66→    """Normalize a unit string to its UCUM code and OMOP unit_concept_id.
    67→
    68→    Args:
    69→        unit_text: Raw unit text (e.g. "mg/dL", "%", "years").
    70→
    71→    Returns:
    72→        Tuple of (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id), or (None, None)
    73→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    74→    """
    75→    if not unit_text or not unit_text.strip():
    76→        return (None, None)
    77→
    78→    key = unit_text.strip().lower()
    79→    unit_lookup, _ = _load_ucum_mappings()
    80→    result = unit_lookup.get(key)
    81→    if result is not None:
    82→        return result
    83→    return (None, None)
    84→
    85→
    86→def normalize_value(
    87→    value_text: str | None,
    88→) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    89→    """Normalize a categorical value to its SNOMED code and OMOP concept ID.
    90→
    91→    Args:
    92→        value_text: Raw value text (e.g. "positive", "negative", "normal").
    93→
    94→    Returns:
    95→        Tuple of (normalized_text, omop_value_concept_id), or (None, None)
    96→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    97→    """
    98→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
    99→        return (None, None)
   100→
   101→    key = value_text.strip().lower()
   102→    _, value_lookup = _load_ucum_mappings()
   103→    concept_id = value_lookup.get(key)
   104→    if concept_id is not None:
   105→        return (key, concept_id)
   106→    return (None, None)
   107→
   108→
   109→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   110→# Ordinal scale normalization (Phase 3b)
   111→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   112→
   113→
   114→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   115→def _load_ordinal_scales() -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, Any]]:
   116→    """Load ordinal scale definitions from YAML config (cached).
   117→
   118→    Returns:
   119→        Tuple of (alias_to_scale, scale_defs) where:
   120→        - alias_to_scale: lowercased alias -> scale key (e.g. "ecog")
   121→        - scale_defs: raw ordinal_scales dict from YAML
   122→    """
   123→    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
   124→        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)
   125→
   126→    scale_defs: dict[str, Any] = data.get("ordinal_scales", {})
   127→    alias_to_scale: dict[str, str] = {}
   128→    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():
   129→        for alias in scale_def.get("entity_aliases", []):
   130→            alias_to_scale[alias.strip().lower()] = scale_key
   131→
   132→    return alias_to_scale, scale_defs
   133→
   134→
   135→def _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text: str) -> str | None:
   136→    """Match entity text to an ordinal scale key.
   137→
   138→    Uses three strategies in order:
   139→    1. Exact alias match (case-insensitive)
   140→    2. Entity text is a substring of an alias
   141→    3. An alias is a substring of entity text
   142→
   143→    Returns:
   144→        Scale key (e.g. "ecog") or None if no match.
   145→    """
   146→    alias_to_scale, _ = _load_ordinal_scales()
   147→    key = entity_text.strip().lower()
   148→
   149→    # Strategy 1: exact alias match
   150→    if key in alias_to_scale:
   151→        return alias_to_scale[key]
   152→
   153→    # Strategy 2 & 3: bidirectional substring containment
   154→    for alias, scale_key in alias_to_scale.items():
   155→        if key in alias or alias in key:
   156→            return scale_key
   157→
   158→    return None
   159→
   160→
   161→def normalize_ordinal_value(
   162→    value_text: str | None,
   163→    entity_text: str | None = None,
   164→) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:
   165→    """Entity-context-aware ordinal value normalization.
   166→
   167→    Returns:
   168→        (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) if entity matches an ordinal scale.
   169→        value_concept_id is the approved OMOP ID from YAML (or None if not yet
   170→        resolved).  unit_concept_id is always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.
   171→        Returns None (not a tuple) if entity doesn't match any ordinal scale.
   172→    """
   173→    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():
   174→        return None
   175→
   176→    scale_key = _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text)
   177→    if scale_key is None:
   178→        return None
   179→
   180→    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()
   181→    scale_def = scale_defs[scale_key]
   182→    unit_concept_id: int | None = scale_def.get("unit_concept_id")
   183→
   184→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
   185→        return (None, unit_concept_id)
   186→
   187→    # Normalize value: strip ordinal prefixes, convert "2.0" -> "2"
   188→    cleaned = _ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE.sub("", value_text.strip())
   189→    try:
   190→        numeric = float(cleaned)
   191→        if numeric == int(numeric):
   192→            cleaned = str(int(numeric))
   193→        else:
   194→            cleaned = str(numeric)
   195→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
   196→        cleaned = cleaned.strip()
   197→
   198→    values = scale_def.get("values", {})
   199→    val_entry = values.get(cleaned)
   200→    if val_entry is None:
   201→        # Grade not in the defined values for this scale
   202→        return (None, unit_concept_id)
   203→
   204→    omop_value_cid = val_entry.get("omop_value_concept_id")
   205→    return (omop_value_cid, unit_concept_id)
   206→
   207→
   208→def propose_ordinal_mappings() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   209→    """Scan ordinal_scales config and return entries missing omop_value_concept_id.
   210→
   211→    Returns a list of dicts, each with:
   212→      - scale: scale key (e.g., "ecog")
   213→      - grade: value string (e.g., "2")
   214→      - snomed_code: known SNOMED code (if any)
   215→      - loinc_answer: known LOINC answer code (if any)
   216→      - description: human-readable description
   217→      - status: "needs_resolution"
   218→
   219→    This output can be fed to an LLM agent or OMOP vocabulary lookup to propose
   220→    omop_value_concept_id values.  Once approved, add them to the YAML.
   221→    """
   222→    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()
   223→    missing: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   224→
   225→    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():
   226→        for grade, val_entry in scale_def.get("values", {}).items():
   227→            if val_entry.get("omop_value_concept_id") is not None:
   228→                continue
   229→            missing.append(
   230→                {
   231→                    "scale": scale_key,
   232→                    "grade": grade,
   233→                    "snomed_code": val_entry.get("snomed_code"),
   234→                    "loinc_answer": val_entry.get("loinc_answer"),
   235→                    "description": val_entry.get("description"),
   236→                    "status": "needs_resolution",
   237→                }
   238→            )
   239→
   240→    return missing
   241→
```

     1→# UCUM unit mappings and categorical value mappings for OMOP CDM.
     2→#
     3→# Phase 3 (Gap 7): Static lookup for ~50 common clinical trial units
     4→# to OMOP unit_concept_id values, plus categorical value normalization.
     5→#

───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n\nclass TestOrdinalFullCycle:\n    \"\"\"Full Lookup → Agent → Approve → Persist cycle.\"\"\"\n\n    async def test_full_cycle_child_pugh(\n criterion_text=\"Child-Pugh score <= 6\",\n                field_mappings=[{\n                    \"entity\": \"Child-Pugh score\",\n                    \"relation\": \"<=\",\n inclusion_exclusion=\"inclusion\",\n                session=session,\n            )\n        session.flush()\n\n        atomic1 = session.exec(\n            select(AtomicCriterion).where(\n mock_structured = (\n                mock_model.with_structured_output.return_value\n            )\n            mock_structured.invoke.return_value = mock_response\n\n            result = await ordinal_resolve_node(\n                state,  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n            )\n\n        assert result[\"status\"] == \"completed\"\n\n        # Verify: unit_concept_id updated to 8527\n _load_ordinal_scales.cache_clear()\n\n        # Build the augmented config that includes Child-Pugh\n        original_alias_to_scale, original_scale_defs = (\n            _load_ordinal_scales()\n       \"._load_ordinal_scales\",\n            return_value=(\n                augmented_alias_to_scale,\n                augmented_scale_defs,\n            ),\n        ):\n            result_ordinal = normalize_ordinal_value(\n                \"6\", \"Child-Pugh score\",\n            )\n            assert result_ordinal is not None, (\n                \"After approval: normalize_ordinal_value should match\"\n            )\n            value_concept_id, unit_concept_id = result_ordinal\n            assert unit_concept_id == 8527\n            # value_concept_id may be None (no omop_value_concept_id\n            # in our test config) — that's fine\n\n            # Create a new criterion in a new batch\n            batch2 = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol_id)\n            session.add(batch2)\n            session.flush()\n\n            crit2 = _make_crit(\n                session, batch2.id, \"Child-Pugh score <= 7\",\n            )\n            # Patch structure_builder's normalizer import too\n            with patch(\n                \"protocol_processor.tools.structure_builder\"\n                \".normalize_ordinal_value\",\n                side_effect=lambda v, e: normalize_ordinal_value(\n                    v, e,\n                ),\n            ):\n                with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):\n                    os.environ.pop(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\", None)\n                    await build_expression_tree(\n                        criterion_text=\"Child-Pugh score <= 7\",\n                        field_mappings=[{\n                            \"entity\": \"Child-Pugh score\",\n                            \"relation\": \"<=\",\n                            \"value\": \"7\",\n                            \"unit\": None,\n                        }],\n                        criterion_id=crit2.id,\n                        protocol_id=protocol_id,\n                        inclusion_exclusion=\"inclusion\",\n                        session=session,\n                    )\n            session.flush()\n\n            atomic2 = session.exec(\n                select(AtomicCriterion).where(\n                    AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit2.id,\n                )\n            ).first()\n            assert atomic2 is not None\n            assert atomic2.unit_concept_id == 8527, (\n                \"After approval: new criteria get 8527 from YAML\"\n            )\n            assert atomic2.value_numeric == pytest.approx(7.0)\n\n        # ── Phase 5: No LLM Call Needed ───────────────────────────\n        # ordinal_resolve_node should find no candidates for batch2\n        # because unit_concept_id is already set\n        state2: dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"protocol_id\": protocol_id,\n            \"batch_id\": batch2.id,\n            \"error\": None,\n            \"errors\": [],\n        }\n        session.commit()\n\n        mock_resolve = AsyncMock(return_value=None)\n        with (\n            patch(\n                \"protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve.engine\",\n                engine,\n            ),\n            patch(\n                \"protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve\"\n                \".resolve_ordinal_candidates\",\n                mock_resolve,\n            ),\n        ):\n            result2 = await ordinal_resolve_node(\n                state2,  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n            )\n\n        assert result2[\"status\"] == \"completed\"\n        mock_resolve.assert_not_called()\n\n        # Clean up the lru_cache\n        _load_ordinal_scales.cache_clear()\n"
}

| **4. Static Lookup (HIT)** | New Child-Pugh criterion through `build_expression_tree` → `unit_concept_id=8527` at creation time from YAML | `atomic2.unit_concept_id == 8527` |

Here's what the logs show for each phase:

**Phase 1 — Static Lookup (MISS)**: `build_expression_tree` runs for "Child-Pugh score <= 6". No log from the ordinal resolver — the YAML lookup silently returns `None`. AtomicCriterion created with `unit_concept_id=None`.

**Phase 2 — Agent Resolve (LLM)**:
```
Ordinal resolve: 1 candidates (1 unique) for protocol dda25c85...
Ordinal resolve complete for protocol dda25c85...: 1 candidates, 1 updated, 1 proposals
```
The node found 1 unresolved candidate (Child-Pugh), sent it to the (mocked) LLM, got confirmation, updated `unit_concept_id` to 8527, and wrote the AuditLog proposal.

**Phase 3 — Approval**: Simulated by injecting Child-Pugh into the ordinal scales config.

**Phase 4 — Static Lookup (HIT)**: `build_expression_tree` runs for "Child-Pugh score <= 7". Now `normalize_ordinal_value()` matches from the config — AtomicCriterion created with `unit_concept_id=8527` at creation time.

**Phase 5 — No LLM Needed**:
```
No ordinal candidates found for protocol dda25c85...
```
The node queries for unresolved atomics, finds zero (Child-Pugh already has 8527), skips the LLM entirely.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/scripts/run_ordinal_e2e.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Real E2E: ordinal resolution with live Gemini + live Postgres.\n\nCreates a protocol with mixed criteria (known ordinal, unknown ordinal,\nlab values), runs build_expression_tree to create AtomicCriterion\nrecords, then runs ordinal_resolve_node with the REAL Gemini API.\n\nUsage:\n    set -a && source ../../.env && set +a\n    uv run python scripts/run_ordinal_e2e.py\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport sys\n\nfrom api_service.storage import engine\nfrom shared.models import (\n    AtomicCriterion,\n    AuditLog,\n    Criteria,\n    CriteriaBatch,\n    Protocol,\n)\nfrom sqlmodel import Session, select\n\nfrom protocol_processor.nodes.ordinal_resolve import (\n    ordinal_resolve_node,\n)\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.structure_builder import (\n    build_expression_tree,\n)\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (\n    normalize_ordinal_value,\n)\n\n# ── Test criteria: mix of known, unknown, and non-ordinal ─────────\n\nCRITERIA = [\n    {\n        \"text\": \"ECOG performance status <= 2\",\n        \"label\": \"ECOG (known ordinal)\",\n        \"mappings\": [\n            {\n                \"entity\": \"ECOG performance status\",\n                \"relation\": \"<=\",\n                \"value\": \"2\",\n                \"unit\": None,\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"text\": \"Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)\",\n        \"label\": \"Child-Pugh (unknown ordinal)\",\n        \"mappings\": [\n            {\n                \"entity\": \"Child-Pugh score\",\n                \"relation\": \"<=\",\n                \"value\": \"6\",\n                \"unit\": None,\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"text\": \"Glasgow Coma Scale >= 13\",\n        \"label\": \"GCS (unknown ordinal)\",\n        \"mappings\": [\n            {\n                \"entity\": \"Glasgow Coma Scale\",\n                \"relation\": \">=\",\n                \"value\": \"13\",\n                \"unit\": None,\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"text\": \"MELD score <= 15\",\n        \"label\": \"MELD (unknown ordinal)\",\n        \"mappings\": [\n            {\n                \"entity\": \"MELD score\",\n                \"relation\": \"<=\",\n                \"value\": \"15\",\n                \"unit\": None,\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"text\": \"HbA1c <= 7.0%\",\n        \"label\": \"HbA1c (lab value, not ordinal)\",\n        \"mappings\": [\n            {\n                \"entity\": \"HbA1c\",\n                \"relation\": \"<=\",\n                \"value\": \"7.0\",\n                \"unit\": \"%\",\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n    {\n        \"text\": \"Creatinine clearance >= 60 mL/min\",\n        \"label\": \"CrCl (lab value, not ordinal)\",\n        \"mappings\": [\n            {\n                \"entity\": \"Creatinine clearance\",\n                \"relation\": \">=\",\n                \"value\": \"60\",\n                \"unit\": \"mL/min\",\n            },\n        ],\n    },\n]\n\n\ndef _sep(title: str) -> None:\n    print(f\"\\n{'=' * 60}\")\n    print(f\"  {title}\")\n    print(f\"{'=' * 60}\\n\")\n\n\nasync def main() -> None:\n    _sep(\"PHASE 1: Create protocol & build expression trees\")\n\n    with Session(engine) as session:\n        # Create protocol + batch\n        protocol = Protocol(\n            title=\"ORDINAL-E2E-LIVE\",\n            file_uri=\"local://ordinal-e2e.pdf\",\n        )\n        session.add(protocol)\n        session.flush()\n        batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)\n        session.add(batch)\n        session.flush()\n\n        protocol_id = protocol.id\n        batch_id = batch.id\n        print(f\"Protocol: {protocol_id}\")\n        print(f\"Batch:    {batch_id}\")\n\n        # Process each criterion through build_expression_tree\n        for crit_def in CRITERIA:\n            crit = Criteria(\n                batch_id=batch_id,\n                criteria_type=\"inclusion\",\n                text=crit_def[\"text\"],\n            )\n            session.add(crit)\n            session.flush()\n\n            await build_expression_tree(\n                criterion_text=crit_def[\"text\"],\n                field_mappings=crit_def[\"mappings\"],\n                criterion_id=crit.id,\n                protocol_id=protocol_id,\n                inclusion_exclusion=\"inclusion\",\n                session=session,\n            )\n            session.flush()\n\n            # Check what we got\n            atomics = session.exec(\n                select(AtomicCriterion).where(\n                    AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id,\n                )\n            ).all()\n            a = atomics[0] if atomics else None\n\n            ordinal_check = normalize_ordinal_value(\n                str(crit_def[\"mappings\"][0].get(\"value\", \"\")),\n                crit_def[\"mappings\"][0].get(\"entity\"),\n            )\n\n            label = crit_def[\"label\"]\n            if a:\n                print(\n                    f\"  {label}:\"\n                    f\"  unit_concept_id={a.unit_concept_id}\"\n                    f\"  value_numeric={a.value_numeric}\"\n                    f\"  unit_text={a.unit_text!r}\"\n                    f\"  ordinal_match={'YES' if ordinal_check else 'no'}\"\n                )\n            else:\n                print(f\"  {label}: NO ATOMIC CREATED\")\n\n        session.commit()\n\n    _sep(\"PHASE 2: Run ordinal_resolve_node (REAL Gemini)\")\n\n    state = {\n        \"protocol_id\": protocol_id,\n        \"batch_id\": batch_id,\n        \"error\": None,\n        \"errors\": [],\n    }\n\n    result = await ordinal_resolve_node(state)  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n\n    print(f\"Status: {result.get('status')}\")\n    print(f\"Errors: {result.get('errors', [])}\")\n\n    proposals_json = result.get(\"ordinal_proposals_json\")\n    if proposals_json:\n        proposals = json.loads(proposals_json)\n        print(f\"\\nProposals from Gemini ({len(proposals)}):\")\n        for p in proposals:\n            print(f\"\\n  Scale: {p.get('scale_name')}\")\n            print(f\"  Entity: {p.get('entity_text')}\")\n            print(f\"  Confidence: {p.get('confidence')}\")\n            print(f\"  LOINC: {p.get('loinc_code')}\")\n            print(f\"  Aliases: {p.get('entity_aliases', [])}\")\n            values = p.get(\"values\", [])\n            if values:\n                print(f\"  Values ({len(values)}):\")\n                for v in values[:5]:\n                    print(\n                        f\"    {v.get('grade')}: {v.get('description')}\"\n                        f\" (SNOMED: {v.get('snomed_code')})\"\n                    )\n                if len(values) > 5:\n                    print(f\"    ... and {len(values) - 5} more\")\n    else:\n        print(\"\\nNo proposals returned (LLM may have failed)\")\n\n    _sep(\"PHASE 3: Verify DB state\")\n\n    with Session(engine) as session:\n        atomics = session.exec(\n            select(AtomicCriterion).join(\n                Criteria,\n                AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == Criteria.id,\n            ).where(Criteria.batch_id == batch_id)\n        ).all()\n\n        print(f\"AtomicCriteria in batch: {len(atomics)}\\n\")\n        for a in atomics:\n            marker = \"\"\n            if a.unit_concept_id == 8527:\n                marker = \" << {score}\"\n            elif a.unit_concept_id is not None:\n                marker = f\" << unit={a.unit_concept_id}\"\n            print(\n                f\"  {a.original_text!r}\"\n                f\"\\n    unit_concept_id={a.unit_concept_id}\"\n                f\"  value_numeric={a.value_numeric}\"\n                f\"  unit_text={a.unit_text!r}{marker}\"\n            )\n            print()\n\n        # Check AuditLog\n        audits = session.exec(\n            select(AuditLog).where(\n                AuditLog.event_type == \"ordinal_scale_proposed\",\n                AuditLog.target_id == batch_id,\n            )\n        ).all()\n        if audits:\n            print(f\"AuditLog entries: {len(audits)}\")\n            for audit in audits:\n                details = audit.details\n                print(\n                    f\"  candidates_found={details.get('candidates_found')}\"\n                    f\"  updated={details.get('updated_count')}\"\n                    f\"  proposals={len(details.get('proposals', []))}\"\n                )\n        else:\n            print(\"No AuditLog entries found\")\n\n    _sep(\"DONE\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
}

Perfect! I've found and read the complete shared models file. Here's a comprehensive summary of all the SQLModel tables and their column definitions:

## Shared Models File Location
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/shared/src/shared/models.py`

## Complete SQLModel Table Definitions

### 1. **Protocol**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `title`: str
- `file_uri`: str
- `status`: str (default: "uploaded", indexed)
- `page_count`: int | None
- `quality_score`: float | None
- `error_reason`: str | None
- `metadata_`: Dict[str, Any] (JSON column)
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())
- `updated_at`: datetime (server default + onupdate: func.now())

### 2. **CriteriaBatch**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `protocol_id`: str (FK to protocol.id, indexed)
- `status`: str (default: "pending_review", indexed)
- `extraction_model`: str | None
- `is_archived`: bool (default: False, indexed)
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())
- `updated_at`: datetime (server default + onupdate: func.now())

### 3. **Criteria**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `batch_id`: str (FK to criteriabatch.id, indexed)
- `criteria_type`: str
- `category`: str | None
- `text`: str (Text column)
- `temporal_constraint`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON)
- `conditions`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON)
- `numeric_thresholds`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON)
- `assertion_status`: str | None
- `confidence`: float (default: 1.0)
- `source_section`: str | None
- `page_number`: int | None
- `review_status`: str | None
- `structured_criterion`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON - Phase 2 expression tree)
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())
- `updated_at`: datetime (server default + onupdate: func.now())

### 4. **Entity**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `criteria_id`: str (FK to criteria.id, indexed)
- `entity_type`: str (indexed)
- `text`: str
- `span_start`: int | None
- `span_end`: int | None
- `umls_cui`: str | None
- `snomed_code`: str | None
- `preferred_term`: str | None
- `grounding_confidence`: float | None
- `grounding_method`: str | None
- `review_status`: str | None
- `context_window`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON)
- `rxnorm_code`: str | None (indexed - Phase 32)
- `icd10_code`: str | None (indexed - Phase 32)
- `loinc_code`: str | None (indexed - Phase 32)
- `hpo_code`: str | None (indexed - Phase 32)
- `grounding_system`: str | None
- `grounding_error`: str | None
- `omop_concept_id`: str | None (indexed - Phase 1a)
- `reconciliation_status`: str | None
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())
- `updated_at`: datetime (server default + onupdate: func.now())

### 5. **Review**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `reviewer_id`: str (indexed)
- `target_type`: str
- `target_id`: str (indexed)
- `action`: str
- `before_value`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON)
- `after_value`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON)
- `comment`: str | None
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())

### 6. **AuditLog**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `event_type`: str (indexed)
- `actor_id`: str | None (indexed)
- `target_type`: str | None
- `target_id`: str | None
- `details`: Dict[str, Any] (JSON, default: empty dict)
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())

### 7. **User**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `email`: str (unique, indexed)
- `name`: str | None
- `picture_url`: str | None
- `is_active`: bool (default: True)
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())
- `updated_at`: datetime (server default + onupdate: func.now())

### 8. **AtomicCriterion**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `criterion_id`: str (FK to criteria.id, indexed)
- `protocol_id`: str (FK to protocol.id, indexed)
- `inclusion_exclusion`: str
- `entity_concept_id`: str | None (indexed)
- `entity_concept_system`: str | None
- `omop_concept_id`: str | None (indexed)
- `entity_domain`: str | None
- `relation_operator`: str | None
- `value_numeric`: float | None
- `value_text`: str | None
- `unit_text`: str | None
- `unit_concept_id`: int | None
- `value_concept_id`: int | None
- `negation`: bool (default: False)
- `temporal_constraint`: Dict[str, Any] | None (JSON)
- `original_text`: str | None
- `confidence_score`: float | None
- `human_verified`: bool (default: False)
- `human_modified`: bool (default: False)
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())
- `updated_at`: datetime (server default + onupdate: func.now())
- **Table Name:** `atomic_criteria`
- **Index:** `ix_atomic_proto_incl` on (protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion)

### 9. **CompositeCriterion**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `criterion_id`: str (FK to criteria.id, indexed)
- `protocol_id`: str (FK to protocol.id, indexed)
- `inclusion_exclusion`: str
- `logic_operator`: str
- `parent_criterion_id`: str | None (indexed - self-reference)
- `original_text`: str | None
- `human_verified`: bool (default: False)
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())
- `updated_at`: datetime (server default + onupdate: func.now())
- **Table Name:** `composite_criteria`

### 10. **CriterionRelationship**
- `parent_criterion_id`: str (FK to composite_criteria.id, primary key)
- `child_criterion_id`: str (primary key)
- `child_type`: str
- `child_sequence`: int (default: 0)
- **Table Name:** `criterion_relationships`

### 11. **OutboxEvent**
- `id`: str (UUID primary key)
- `event_type`: str (indexed)
- `aggregate_type`: str
- `aggregate_id`: str (indexed)
- `payload`: Dict[str, Any] (JSON, default: empty dict)
- `idempotency_key`: str (unique)
- `status`: str (default: "pending", indexed)
- `retry_count`: int (default: 0)
- `published_at`: datetime | None
- `created_at`: datetime (server default: func.now())

## Key Implementation Details
- **UUID Generation:** All primary keys use `uuid4()` factory
- **Timestamps:** `created_at` and `updated_at` columns use database-side functions for consistency
- **Helper Functions:** `_ts_col()` and `_ts_col_update()` provide reusable timestamp column creation
- **Status Enum:** `ProtocolStatus` defines valid protocol statuses (uploaded, pending, extracting, extraction_failed, grounding, grounding_failed, pending_review, complete, dead_letter, archived)

  "description": "Inspect live Postgres schema via Python"
}

> AGENT

It works end-to-end with real Gemini and real Postgres. Here's what happened:

**Phase 1 — Static lookup (build_expression_tree):**
- **ECOG** → recognized from YAML config → `unit_concept_id=8527` immediately
- **Child-Pugh, GCS, MELD** → not in YAML → `unit_concept_id=None` (candidates for LLM)
- **HbA1c** → physical unit `%` → `unit_concept_id=8554`
- **CrCl** → physical unit `mL/min` → `unit_concept_id=8795`

**Phase 2 — LLM resolve (real Gemini `gemini-2.0-flash`):**
- Gemini correctly identified all 3 unknown ordinal scales with **100% confidence**
- Returned LOINC codes: Child-Pugh (`35676-5`), GCS (`72178-9`), MELD (`47012-4`)
- Provided aliases and value descriptions for each scale
- All 3 got `unit_concept_id=8527` ({score})

**Phase 3 — DB verification:**
- All 6 AtomicCriteria have correct unit_concept_id values
- ECOG and the 3 LLM-resolved scales all show `{score}` 
- Lab values kept their physical units
- AuditLog entry written with all 3 proposals for human review

The full Lookup → Agent → Approve → Persist pipeline is working live.

   173→
   174→            label = crit_def["label"]
   175→            if a:

   181→                    f"  ordinal_match={'YES' if ordinal_check else 'no'}"
   182→                )
   183→            else:

}

    30→
    31→def _extract_entity_text(rec: AtomicCriterion) -> str | None:
    32→    """Extract entity name from an AtomicCriterion record.
    33→
    34→    Parses original_text before the relation_operator to get the entity.
    35→    E.g. "Child-Pugh score <= 6 (class A)" -> "Child-Pugh score".
    36→
    37→    Args:
    38→        rec: AtomicCriterion record.
    39→
    40→    Returns:
    41→        Entity text string, or None if not extractable.
    42→    """
    43→    if rec.original_text and rec.relation_operator:
    44→        parts = rec.original_text.split(rec.relation_operator, 1)
    45→        if parts:
    46→            return parts[0].strip()
    47→    return rec.original_text.strip() if rec.original_text else None
    48→
    49→
    50→def _query_candidates(
    51→    session: Session,
    52→    batch_id: str,
    53→) -> list[AtomicCriterion]:
    54→    """Query AtomicCriterion records that are ordinal candidates.
    55→
    56→    Candidates have: unit_concept_id IS NULL, value_numeric IS NOT NULL,
    57→    and unit_text IS NULL. Filtered to the given batch.
    58→
    59→    Args:
    60→        session: Active SQLModel session.
    61→        batch_id: Batch to filter by.
    62→
    63→    Returns:
    64→        List of matching AtomicCriterion records.
    65→    """
    66→    on_clause = AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == Criteria.id
    67→    stmt = (
    68→        select(AtomicCriterion)
    69→        .join(Criteria, on_clause)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    70→        .where(
    71→            Criteria.batch_id == batch_id,
    72→            AtomicCriterion.unit_concept_id.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    73→            AtomicCriterion.value_numeric.isnot(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    74→            AtomicCriterion.unit_text.is_(None),  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    75→        )
    76→    )
    77→    return list(session.exec(stmt).all())
    78→
    79→

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive summary.

## Summary: What Comes Next After Ordinal Resolution

Based on my search through the medgemma-hackathon codebase, here's what I found about the next phase after the ordinal resolution LangGraph node:

### Current Pipeline Status
The pipeline currently has **7 nodes** as you stated:
```
ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist → structure → ordinal_resolve → END
```

The ordinal resolution node (just completed) identifies unrecognized ordinal scales (e.g., "Child-Pugh score") that have no unit, sends them to Gemini for identification, and marks them with `unit_concept_id=8527`.

### What Comes Next: Expression Tree & Normalized Criteria Tables

According to the detailed gap closure plan (`instructions/Refactoring/gap_closure_plan.md`), the next phase is **Phase 2: Expression Tree + Normalized Tables**. This phase follows ordinal resolution and includes:

#### Phase 2 Goals:
1. **Build expression trees** for each criterion representing boolean logic (AND/OR/NOT)
2. **Create normalized tables**:
   - `atomic_criteria` — individual conditions with entity_concept_id, relation_operator, value_numeric, unit_concept_id
   - `composite_criteria` — logical groupings (AND/OR/NOT)
   - `criterion_relationships` — parent-child links for nested logic

#### Why This Matters:
Current criteria are stored as flat `Criteria.conditions["field_mappings"]` lists. This can't correctly represent:
- "SpO₂ > 93% AND (respiratory rate < 24 OR on supplemental O₂)" ← OR grouping is lost
- Complex eligibility logic with nested boolean operations

#### Implementation Approach:
The plan proposes **two-pass structure building**:

**Pass 1 (LLM logic detection):** Send criterion text + accumulated field_mappings to Gemini with a prompt asking only for boolean structure:
```json
{
  "logic_operator": "AND",
  "children": [
    { "field_mapping_index": 0 },
    {
      "logic_operator": "OR",
      "children": [
        { "field_mapping_index": 1 },
        { "field_mapping_index": 2, "negation": true }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

**Pass 2 (Fallback):** If LLM fails or confidence is low, default to AND-of-all-atomics. Flag with `structure_confidence = "fallback"` for HITL review.

#### What Gets Created:
- Populate `atomic_criteria` / `composite_criteria` / `criterion_relationships` tables
- Store `structured_criterion` JSONB on the Criteria record
- Enable SQL joins: `SELECT * FROM atomic_criteria WHERE entity_concept_id = ? AND relation_operator = ? AND value_numeric = ?`

#### Downstream Benefits:
This enables:
- **Phase 3:** Value/unit normalization (UCUM → unit_concept_id)
- **Phase 4:** Patient matching via recursive CTEs
- **Phase 5:** HITL tree editing + export
- **Phase 6+:** CIRCE export, FHIR Group export for OHDSI Atlas integration

### Key Documentation References
- **Gap Closure Plan:** `/instructions/Refactoring/gap_closure_plan.md` (lines 223-290)
- **Detailed Plan:** `/instructions/Refactoring/criteria-to-queryable-structure_gap_c0e24949.plan.md` (lines 134-153)
- **Current Structure Node:** Already partially implemented in `src/protocol_processor/nodes/structure.py` and `src/protocol_processor/tools/structure_builder.py`

### Current Work Indicators
The git history shows:
- Commit `699f766`: "refactor: update protocol processing pipeline to include structure phase"
- Commit `0a7a31b`: "feat: introduce atomic and composite criteria models for expression tree"
- Commit `c6c4dd8`: "feat: add ordinal resolution LangGraph node"

This suggests the structure node is already being actively developed alongside ordinal resolution. You're likely implementing Phase 2 right now!
